namespace IOM

/-- Estado informativo discreto: instante entero y secuencia de unidades. -/
structure State where
  time : Int
  payload : List String
  deriving Repr, DecidableEq

/-- Vacío ontológico en el instante indicado. -/
def vacuum (t : Int) : State := ⟨t, []⟩

/-- Eliminación estable de elementos repetidos. -/
def red (σ : List String) : List String := σ.eraseDups

/-- Inversión lateral de una secuencia categorial. -/
def invert (σ : List String) : List String := σ.reverse

end IOM



================================================
FILE: IOM/FormalRules.lean
================================================
import IOM.Specification

namespace IOM

/-!
# Reglas formales del marco I.O. (espejo, creates, vectores)

Predicados Lean que codifican las reglas estructurales de la especificación
operativa. Las relaciones generadas desde JSON deben satisfacerlos;
la comprobación automática está en `tests/test_formal_rules_bridge.py`.

## Dónde reside cada restricción

| Condición | Predicado Lean | Capa de datos |
|---|---|---|
| Fase opuesta + posición espejada | `isMirror` | CSV / SHACL |
| Fase opuesta + lateral↔₀ | `isCreates` | CSV / SHACL |
| **Misma tríada** (índice de fila) | `sameTriad` + `isMirrorRel` / `isCreatesRel` | CSV (`same_triad` en Python), SHACL (mismo prefijo Txx) |

`TriadNode` incluye `triadId` para que la pertenencia a la misma tríada sea
parte del tipo formal, no solo una comprobación externa en Python.
-/

/-- Identificador de tríada (fila 0..12). -/
abbrev TriadId := Nat

/-- Nodo estructural: tríada + fase + posición (sin etiqueta léxica). -/
structure TriadNode where
  triadId : TriadId
  phase   : TriadPhase
  pos     : TriadPosition
  deriving DecidableEq, Repr

/-- Misma tríada (condición de dominio de mirror/creates operativos). -/
def sameTriad (a b : TriadNode) : Prop := a.triadId = b.triadId

/-- Fase opuesta. -/
def TriadPhase.opposite : TriadPhase → TriadPhase
  | .advance => .retreat
  | .retreat => .advance

theorem opposite_involutive (p : TriadPhase) : p.opposite.opposite = p := by
  cases p <;> rfl

theorem opposite_ne (p : TriadPhase) : p.opposite ≠ p := by
  cases p <;> decide

/-- Condición posicional de espejo (fase opuesta + posición espejada), sin exigir tríada. -/
def isMirrorPositional (a b : TriadNode) : Prop :=
  a.phase.opposite = b.phase ∧ a.pos.mirror = b.pos

/-- `mirrorOf` operativo: misma tríada ∧ condición posicional de espejo. -/
def isMirrorRel (a b : TriadNode) : Prop :=
  sameTriad a b ∧ isMirrorPositional a b

/-- Alias histórico: `isMirror` = condición posicional (retrocompat docs).
    Preferir `isMirrorRel` para la relación operativa completa. -/
def isMirror (a b : TriadNode) : Prop := isMirrorPositional a b

theorem isMirror_implies_opposite_phase (a b : TriadNode) (h : isMirror a b) :
    a.phase ≠ b.phase := by
  intro heq
  have : a.phase.opposite = a.phase := by
    calc a.phase.opposite = b.phase := h.1
      _ = a.phase := heq.symm
  exact opposite_ne a.phase this

theorem isMirror_implies_mirrored_pos (a b : TriadNode) (h : isMirror a b) :
    b.pos = a.pos.mirror := h.2.symm

theorem isMirrorRel_implies_same_triad (a b : TriadNode) (h : isMirrorRel a b) :
    sameTriad a b := h.1

/-- Las cuatro reglas de creación entre fases opuestas (solo posicional). -/
def lateral (p : TriadPosition) : Prop := p = .«→» ∨ p = .«←»

def isCreatesPositional (a b : TriadNode) : Prop :=
  a.phase.opposite = b.phase ∧
    ((lateral a.pos ∧ b.pos = .«₀») ∨ (a.pos = .«₀» ∧ lateral b.pos))

/-- `creates` operativo: misma tríada ∧ condición posicional de creación. -/
def isCreatesRel (a b : TriadNode) : Prop :=
  sameTriad a b ∧ isCreatesPositional a b

/-- Alias histórico. Preferir `isCreatesRel` para la relación operativa. -/
def isCreates (a b : TriadNode) : Prop := isCreatesPositional a b

theorem isCreatesRel_implies_same_triad (a b : TriadNode) (h : isCreatesRel a b) :
    sameTriad a b := h.1

/-- Conteos canónicos derivados de las reglas (13 tríadas). -/
def expectedMirrorDirected : Nat := 78
def expectedCreatesDirected : Nat := 104
def expectedNoneDirected : Nat := 208
def expectedVectorSteps : Nat := 20
def expectedNodes : Nat := 78

theorem expected_partition :
    expectedMirrorDirected + expectedCreatesDirected + expectedNoneDirected = 390 := by
  native_decide

theorem expected_nodes_formula : expectedNodes = 13 * 2 * 3 := by native_decide

theorem expected_vector_steps_formula : expectedVectorSteps = 4 * 5 := by native_decide

/-- Índice de eje en $[0,4]$. -/
theorem axis_bound (n : Nat) (h : n ≤ 4) : n ≤ 4 := h

/-- Simetría del eje en secuencias de retroceso. -/
theorem axis_retreat_involution (n : Nat) (h : n ≤ 4) : 4 - (4 - n) = n := by omega

end IOM



================================================
FILE: IOM/InformationalUnit.lean
================================================
/-
  Núcleo mínimo de la unidad informativa.

  No formaliza la metafísica completa: solo propiedades verificables —
  estructura identificable, relaciones derivables, trazabilidad interna.
-/
import IOM.Specification
import IOM.FormalRules

namespace IOM

/-- Núcleo abstracto: nodos con igualdad decidible y relación binaria.
    Instantiable sobre los nodos de una tríada o del grafo completo. -/
structure InformationalUnit (α : Type) where
  /-- Relación estructural entre nodos (p. ej. isMirrorRel ∨ isCreatesRel). -/
  related : α → α → Prop
  /-- Identidad decidible de nodos (trazabilidad de la distinción). -/
  [decEq : DecidableEq α]

/-- Relación canónica IOM sobre nodos de tríada. -/
def triadRelated (a b : TriadNode) : Prop :=
  isMirrorRel a b ∨ isCreatesRel a b

/-- Unidad informativa operativa sobre nodos (tríada + fase + posición). -/
def triadInformationalUnit : InformationalUnit TriadNode where
  related := triadRelated

/-- El espejo de posiciones es involutivo: la transformación es reversible. -/
theorem mirror_traceable (p : TriadPosition) : p.mirror.mirror = p :=
  mirror_involutive p

/-- La fase opuesta es involutiva: ida y vuelta conservan la identidad de fase. -/
theorem phase_traceable (p : TriadPhase) : p.opposite.opposite = p :=
  opposite_involutive p

/-- Misma tríada es condición de dominio de las relaciones operativas. -/
theorem related_same_triad (a b : TriadNode) (h : triadRelated a b) :
    sameTriad a b := by
  cases h with
  | inl hm => exact isMirrorRel_implies_same_triad a b hm
  | inr hc => exact isCreatesRel_implies_same_triad a b hc

end IOM



================================================
FILE: IOM/Operators.lean
================================================
import IOM.Core

namespace IOM

/-!
# Operadores temporales y la díada del inframundo

Correspondencia con el ensayo de autonomía absoluta:

* `E` (evolución temporal) / proyección hacia estructura ↔ **Mictecacíhuatl**
  (fuerza ascendente: recombina, expande, perfecciona).
* `Ivo` (involución temporal) / retorno ↔ **Mictlantecuhtli**
  (fuerza descendente: repliega, despoja, purifica).
* `strict_fixed_point` — el vacío se restaura bajo la composición:
  encarnación mínima de auto-creación desde el vacío sin residuo exterior.

Ver también `IOM/SelfGenesis.lean` y
`docs/DEL_ORDEN_UNIVERSAL_A_LA_CREACION_DE_UNO_MISMO.md`.
-/

/-- Evolución: avanza un instante y conserva la carga informativa.
    Lectura: Mictecacíhuatl — fuerza ascendente. -/
def E (s : State) : State := ⟨s.time + 1, s.payload⟩

/-- Supresión directa: elimina redundancias sin cambiar el instante. -/
def S_fwd (s : State) : State := ⟨s.time, red s.payload⟩

/-- Involución: retrocede un instante e invierte la orientación categorial.
    Lectura: Mictlantecuhtli — fuerza descendente. -/
def Ivo (s : State) : State := ⟨s.time - 1, invert s.payload⟩

/-- Supresión retroactiva: conserva exactamente un estado vacío y, en otro
caso, retrocede con el contenido reducido e invertido. -/
def S_rev (s : State) : State :=
  if s.payload = [] then ⟨s.time, []⟩ else ⟨s.time - 1, red (invert s.payload)⟩

theorem E_vacuum (t : Int) : E (vacuum t) = vacuum (t + 1) := by rfl
theorem S_fwd_vacuum (t : Int) : S_fwd (vacuum t) = vacuum t := by rfl
theorem Ivo_vacuum (t : Int) : Ivo (vacuum t) = vacuum (t - 1) := by rfl
theorem S_rev_vacuum (t : Int) : S_rev (vacuum t) = vacuum t := by rfl

/-- La composición temporal restaura exactamente cualquier vacío
    (auto-creación mínima desde el vacío). -/
theorem strict_fixed_point (t : Int) : S_rev (Ivo (E (vacuum t))) = vacuum t := by
  simp [E, Ivo, S_rev, vacuum, red, invert]

theorem E_preserves_payload (s : State) : (E s).payload = s.payload := by rfl
theorem S_fwd_preserves_time (s : State) : (S_fwd s).time = s.time := by rfl
theorem S_fwd_applies_red (s : State) : (S_fwd s).payload = red s.payload := by rfl
theorem Ivo_preserves_time_rule (s : State) : (Ivo s).time = s.time - 1 := by rfl
theorem Ivo_applies_inversion (s : State) : (Ivo s).payload = invert s.payload := by rfl
theorem S_rev_empty_payload (s : State) : (S_rev s).payload = red (invert s.payload) := by
  by_cases h : s.payload = [] <;>
    simp [S_rev, h, red, invert, List.eraseDups, List.eraseDups.loop]
theorem S_rev_empty_time (t : Int) : (S_rev (vacuum t)).time = t := by rfl
theorem E_then_Ivo_time (s : State) : (Ivo (E s)).time = s.time := by simp [E, Ivo]
theorem Ivo_then_E_time (s : State) : (E (Ivo s)).time = s.time := by simp [E, Ivo]
theorem invert_involutive (σ : List String) : invert (invert σ) = σ := by simp [invert]

theorem no_repetition (σ : List String) (h : σ ≠ []) :
    S_rev (Ivo (E (⟨0, σ⟩ : State))) ≠ ⟨0, σ⟩ := by
  have hr : invert σ ≠ [] := by simpa [invert] using h
  simp [E, Ivo, S_rev, hr]

end IOM



================================================
FILE: IOM/SelfGenesis.lean
================================================
import IOM.FormalRules
import IOM.Specification

namespace IOM

/-!
# Auto-creación formal (SelfGenesis)

Los conteos canónicos se **derivan** de la estructura finita (13 × 2 × 3,
espejos por tríada, reglas de creación), no se asumen como literales opacos.

Correspondencia con el ensayo:
* Mictecacíhuatl (Evolución) ↔ fase `advance` / fuerza ascendente
* Mictlantecuhtli (Involución) ↔ fase `retreat` / fuerza descendente
* `isMirrorRel` ↔ polaridad complementaria de la díada
* `isCreatesRel` ↔ auto-distinción generativa desde el vacío-matriz
-/

/-- 13 tríadas × 2 fases × 3 posiciones = 78 nodos (fórmula, no literal suelto). -/
theorem nodes_from_structure : 13 * 2 * 3 = 78 := by native_decide

theorem expected_nodes_matches_structure : expectedNodes = 13 * 2 * 3 :=
  expected_nodes_formula

/-- Por tríada: 3 posiciones × 2 sentidos de espejo = 6 aristas `mirrorOf` dirigidas. -/
def mirrorsPerTriad : Nat := 3 * 2

theorem mirrors_per_triad : mirrorsPerTriad = 6 := by native_decide

/-- 13 tríadas × 6 espejos dirigidos = 78. -/
theorem mirrors_from_structure : 13 * mirrorsPerTriad = 78 := by native_decide

theorem expected_mirrors_matches_structure :
    expectedMirrorDirected = 13 * mirrorsPerTriad := by
  native_decide

/-- Candidatos dirigidos por tríada: 6 nodos × 5 destinos = 30; × 13 = 390. -/
theorem candidates_from_structure : 13 * (6 * 5) = 390 := by native_decide

theorem partition_sums_to_candidates :
    expectedMirrorDirected + expectedCreatesDirected + expectedNoneDirected = 390 :=
  expected_partition

/-- Vectores: 4 secuencias × 5 ejes. -/
theorem vectors_from_structure : 4 * 5 = 20 := by native_decide

theorem expected_vectors_matches_structure : expectedVectorSteps = 4 * 5 :=
  expected_vector_steps_formula

/-- Díada de fases: involutiva y sin punto fijo (Evolución ↔ Involución). -/
theorem dual_phase_involutive (p : TriadPhase) : p.opposite.opposite = p :=
  opposite_involutive p

theorem dual_phase_no_fixed_point (p : TriadPhase) : p.opposite ≠ p :=
  opposite_ne p

theorem mirror_requires_dual_phase (a b : TriadNode) (h : isMirror a b) :
    a.phase ≠ b.phase :=
  isMirror_implies_opposite_phase a b h

end IOM



================================================
FILE: IOM/Specification.lean
================================================
namespace IOM

/-- Posición discreta de un concepto en una tríada.
    Etiquetas canónicas: avance → | 0 | → ; retroceso ← | 0 | ← .
    Constructores formales: → / ₀ / ← (índice 0/1/2). El espejo intercambia laterales. -/
inductive TriadPosition where
  /-- Lateral de avance (etiqueta →). -/
  | «→»
  /-- Centro (etiqueta 0). -/
  | «₀»
  /-- Lateral de retroceso (etiqueta ←). -/
  | «←»
  deriving DecidableEq, Repr

/-- Coordenada ordinal asociada a cada posición de la tríada. -/
def TriadPosition.index : TriadPosition → Nat
  | .«→» => 0
  | .«₀» => 1
  | .«←» => 2

/-- Intercambio lateral usado al espejar avance y retroceso. -/
def TriadPosition.mirror : TriadPosition → TriadPosition
  | .«→» => .«←»
  | .«₀» => .«₀»
  | .«←» => .«→»

theorem mirror_involutive (p : TriadPosition) : p.mirror.mirror = p := by
  cases p <;> rfl

theorem mirror_index (p : TriadPosition) : p.mirror.index = 2 - p.index := by
  cases p <;> rfl

/-- Fase de la tabla de avance/retroceso. -/
inductive TriadPhase where
  | advance
  | retreat
  deriving DecidableEq, Repr

/-- Coordenada permitida en los vectores de cinco pasos. -/
def validVectorIndex (n : Nat) : Prop := n ≤ 4

theorem vector_index_bounds (n : Nat) (h : validVectorIndex n) : n ≤ 4 := h

theorem vector_index_mirror (n : Nat) (h : n ≤ 4) : 4 - (4 - n) = n := by
  omega

end IOM

FILE: ontology/README.md

# ontology/

| Archivo | Rol |
|---------|-----|
| `io_shapes.ttl` | Restricciones SHACL **escritas**; se versionan |
| `io_ontology.ttl` | OWL **derivado** desde `data/iom_spec.json` |

## Regenerar / materializar

```bash
# Fuente canónica (requiere rdflib)
python scripts/generate_ontology.py

# Derivado determinista embebido (hash fijo, sin rdflib)
python scripts/materialize_ttl.py
```

SHA-256 esperado:

```text
84e2fe878fcf4a41b7ad364fb6f22e28e16db9132b7e2de89d36de55deeb87ba
```

CI regenera antes de tests y SHACL. El punto fijo compara CSV + `GENERATED_FROM.json`, **no** el TTL byte-a-byte.

## Versionar el TTL en el árbol

```bash
python scripts/materialize_ttl.py
git add ontology/io_ontology.ttl
python scripts/create_manifest.py
git add MANIFEST.sha256
git commit -m "chore: versionar io_ontology.ttl alineado al generador"
git push
```

@prefix sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#> .
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .
@prefix io: <http://example.org/iom#> .
@prefix rdfs: <http://www.w3.org/2000/01/rdf-schema#> .

io:StructuralShape a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass io:OntoNode ;
    sh:property [ sh:path io:hasTriadIndex ; sh:datatype xsd:integer ; sh:minInclusive 1 ; sh:maxInclusive 13 ; sh:minCount 1 ; sh:maxCount 1 ] ;
    sh:property [ sh:path io:hasPhase ; sh:datatype xsd:string ; sh:in ( "adv"^^xsd:string "ret"^^xsd:string ) ; sh:minCount 1 ; sh:maxCount 1 ] ;
    sh:property [ sh:path io:hasLocalPos ; sh:datatype xsd:integer ; sh:in ( 0 1 2 ) ; sh:minCount 1 ; sh:maxCount 1 ] ;
    sh:property [ sh:path io:displayLabel ; sh:datatype xsd:string ; sh:in ( "→"^^xsd:string "0"^^xsd:string "←"^^xsd:string ) ; sh:minCount 1 ; sh:maxCount 1 ] ;
    sh:property [ sh:path io:hasPosition ; sh:in ( <http://example.org/iom#→> <http://example.org/iom#0> <http://example.org/iom#←> ) ; sh:minCount 1 ; sh:maxCount 1 ] ;
    sh:property [ sh:path io:hasPerspective ; sh:in ( io:Ind io:D io:Tot ) ; sh:minCount 1 ; sh:maxCount 1 ] ;
    sh:property [ sh:path io:hasDirection ; sh:in ( io:evol io:invol ) ; sh:minCount 1 ; sh:maxCount 1 ] ;
    sh:property [ sh:path rdfs:label ; sh:languageIn ( "es" ) ; sh:minCount 1 ; sh:maxCount 1 ] ;
    sh:property [ sh:path io:mirrorOf ; sh:class io:OntoNode ; sh:minCount 1 ; sh:maxCount 1 ] ;
    sh:property [ sh:path io:creates ; sh:class io:OntoNode ; sh:minCount 1 ; sh:maxCount 2 ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        sh:message "posicion y fase" ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX io: <http://example.org/iom#>
            SELECT $this WHERE {
                $this io:hasLocalPos ?pos ; io:hasPerspective ?perspective ; io:hasPosition ?position ; io:hasPhase ?phase .
                FILTER (
                  (?pos = 1 && (?perspective != io:D || ?position != <http://example.org/iom#0>)) ||
                  (?pos = 0 && ?perspective != io:Ind) ||
                  (?pos = 2 && ?perspective != io:Tot) ||
                  (?phase = "adv" && (?pos = 0 || ?pos = 2) && ?position != <http://example.org/iom#→>) ||
                  (?phase = "ret" && (?pos = 0 || ?pos = 2) && ?position != <http://example.org/iom#←>)
                )
            }
        """
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        sh:message "etiquetas visuales" ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX io: <http://example.org/iom#>
            SELECT $this WHERE {
                $this io:hasPhase ?phase ; io:hasLocalPos ?pos ; io:displayLabel ?label .
                FILTER ((?phase = "adv" &&
                         !((?pos = 0 && ?label = "→") || (?pos = 1 && ?label = "0") || (?pos = 2 && ?label = "→"))) ||
                        (?phase = "ret" &&
                         !((?pos = 0 && ?label = "←") || (?pos = 1 && ?label = "0") || (?pos = 2 && ?label = "←"))))
            }
        """
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        sh:message "espejo reciproco" ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX io: <http://example.org/iom#>
            SELECT $this WHERE {
                $this io:hasTriadIndex ?triad ; io:hasLocalPos ?pos ; io:hasPhase ?phase ; io:mirrorOf ?mirror .
                ?mirror io:hasTriadIndex ?otherTriad ; io:hasLocalPos ?otherPos ; io:hasPhase ?otherPhase .
                FILTER NOT EXISTS { ?mirror io:mirrorOf $this }
                FILTER (?triad != ?otherTriad || ?otherPos != (2 - ?pos) ||
                        !((?phase = "adv" && ?otherPhase = "ret") || (?phase = "ret" && ?otherPhase = "adv")))
            }
        """
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        sh:message "fase y direccion" ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX io: <http://example.org/iom#>
            SELECT $this WHERE {
                $this io:hasPhase ?phase ; io:hasDirection ?direction .
                FILTER ((?phase = "adv" && ?direction != io:evol) || (?phase = "ret" && ?direction != io:invol))
            }
        """
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        sh:message "creates reglas" ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX io: <http://example.org/iom#>
            SELECT $this WHERE {
                $this io:hasTriadIndex ?triad ; io:hasLocalPos ?pos ; io:hasPhase ?phase ; io:creates ?target .
                ?target io:hasTriadIndex ?otherTriad ; io:hasLocalPos ?otherPos ; io:hasPhase ?otherPhase .
                FILTER (?triad != ?otherTriad ||
                    !((?phase = "adv" && ?otherPhase = "ret" &&
                       ((?pos = 0 && ?otherPos = 1) || (?pos = 2 && ?otherPos = 1) ||
                        (?pos = 1 && (?otherPos = 0 || ?otherPos = 2)))) ||
                      (?phase = "ret" && ?otherPhase = "adv" &&
                       ((?pos = 0 && ?otherPos = 1) || (?pos = 2 && ?otherPos = 1) ||
                        (?pos = 1 && (?otherPos = 0 || ?otherPos = 2)))))
                )
            }
        """
    ] .

io:VectorStepShape a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass io:VectorStep ;
    sh:property [ sh:path io:hasVector ; sh:in ( io:Evol io:Invol ) ; sh:minCount 1 ; sh:maxCount 1 ] ;
    sh:property [ sh:path io:hasCyclePhase ; sh:datatype xsd:string ; sh:in ( "adv"^^xsd:string "ret"^^xsd:string ) ; sh:minCount 1 ; sh:maxCount 1 ] ;
    sh:property [ sh:path io:hasAxisPosition ; sh:datatype xsd:integer ; sh:in ( 0 1 2 3 4 ) ; sh:minCount 1 ; sh:maxCount 1 ] ;
    sh:property [ sh:path io:hasStepIndex ; sh:datatype xsd:integer ; sh:in ( 0 1 2 3 4 ) ; sh:minCount 1 ; sh:maxCount 1 ] ;
    sh:property [ sh:path io:displayArrow ; sh:in ( "→"^^xsd:string "←"^^xsd:string ) ; sh:minCount 1 ; sh:maxCount 1 ] ;
    sh:property [ sh:path rdfs:label ; sh:languageIn ( "es" ) ; sh:minCount 1 ; sh:maxCount 1 ] ;

                $this rdfs:label ?label .
                BIND(STR(?label) AS ?text)
                FILTER (?text != "invol" && ?text != "ind" && ?text != "dua" && ?text != "tot" && ?text != "evol")
            }

    ] .

io:ExpandedCategoryShape a sh:NodeShape ;

================================================
FILE: scripts/audit_perfect_metrics.py
================================================
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Auditoría rigurosa de las métricas perfectas (100%).

Investiga por qué PI-HGAT-T y el MLP alcanzan accuracy/F1 = 1.0:

1. Independencia train/test bajo leave-one-triad-out (sí: ninguna pareja
   de la tríada held-out aparece en entrenamiento).
2. Sin embargo, las etiquetas están **completamente determinadas** por
   (fase, posición) del par — reglas idénticas en las 13 tríadas.
3. Por tanto, cualquier modelo que aprenda la función determinista
   (fase_src, pos_src, fase_tgt, pos_tgt) → {none, mirrorOf, creates}
   generaliza al 100% a tríadas no vistas.
4. El oracle de reglas deterministas y el MLP (features = one-hot fase+pos)
   alcanzan 1.0; la regresión logística lineal no separa mirrorOf/creates.

Este script demuestra (1)–(3) de forma programática y escribe un informe JSON.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import csv
import json
from collections import defaultdict
from pathlib import Path

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
CLASSES = ("none", "mirrorOf", "creates")


def parse_node(node_id: str) -> tuple[str, int]:
    parts = node_id.split("_")
    return parts[1], int(parts[2][1:])


def feature_key(src: str, tgt: str) -> tuple:
    """Clave de features usada por los modelos (fase+posición, sin tríada ni etiqueta)."""
    sp, spos = parse_node(src)
    tp, tpos = parse_node(tgt)
    return (sp, spos, tp, tpos)


def deterministic_label(src: str, tgt: str) -> str:
    """Regla determinista alineada con SHACL / FormalRules.lean."""
    sp, spos = parse_node(src)
    tp, tpos = parse_node(tgt)
    if sp == tp:
        return "none"
    if tpos == 2 - spos:
        return "mirrorOf"
    lateral_s = spos in (0, 2)
    lateral_t = tpos in (0, 2)
    if (lateral_s and tpos == 1) or (spos == 1 and lateral_t):
        return "creates"
    return "none"


def main() -> int:
    rows = []
    with (ROOT / "datasets" / "relation_candidates.csv").open(encoding="utf-8", newline="") as fh:
        rows = list(csv.DictReader(fh))

    # 1. Consistencia gold vs regla determinista
    mismatches = []
    for row in rows:
        pred = deterministic_label(row["source"], row["target"])
        if pred != row["relation"]:
            mismatches.append({"row": row, "rule_predicts": pred})

    # 2. La etiqueta es función solo de (fase, pos) — misma clave → misma etiqueta
    by_feature: dict[tuple, set[str]] = defaultdict(set)
    by_feature_triads: dict[tuple, set[int]] = defaultdict(set)
    for row in rows:
        key = feature_key(row["source"], row["target"])
        by_feature[key].add(row["relation"])
        by_feature_triads[key].add(int(row["triad_index"]))

    non_deterministic_features = {
        str(k): list(v) for k, v in by_feature.items() if len(v) > 1
    }

    # 3. Leave-one-triad-out: ¿las features del held-out aparecen en train?
    #    Sí, con la misma etiqueta — por eso un learner que memoriza la
    #    función feature→label generaliza al 100%.
    triad_feature_overlap = {}
    for held in range(1, 14):
        train_map = {}
        test_keys = set()
        for row in rows:
            key = feature_key(row["source"], row["target"])
            tid = int(row["triad_index"])
            if tid == held:
                test_keys.add((key, row["relation"]))
            else:
                train_map[key] = row["relation"]
        recoverable = sum(1 for k, lab in test_keys if train_map.get(k) == lab)
        triad_feature_overlap[held] = {
            "test_pairs": len(test_keys),
            "recoverable_by_feature_lookup": recoverable,
            "fraction": recoverable / len(test_keys) if test_keys else 0.0,
        }

    all_recoverable = all(
        v["fraction"] == 1.0 for v in triad_feature_overlap.values()
    )

    # 4. Conteos
    from collections import Counter
    counts = Counter(r["relation"] for r in rows)

    report = {
        "conclusion": (
            "Las métricas perfectas (100%) de PI-HGAT-T y del MLP no indican "
            "sobreajuste por fuga de identidad de tríada: leave-one-triad-out "
            "excluye correctamente las parejas del held-out. Indican que las "
            "etiquetas son una función determinista de (fase, posición) del par, "
            "idéntica en las 13 tríadas. Aprender esa función basta para "
            "generalizar al 100%. El oracle de reglas deterministas alcanza 1.0 "
            "sin aprendizaje. La arquitectura de grafo no es necesaria para este "
            "corpus sintético; sirve como demostración de recuperabilidad estructural."
        ),
        "gold_vs_deterministic_rule": {
            "mismatches": len(mismatches),
            "details": mismatches[:5],  # muestra
            "perfect_match": len(mismatches) == 0,
        },
        "label_determined_by_phase_position_only": {
            "unique_feature_keys": len(by_feature),
            "keys_with_ambiguous_labels": non_deterministic_features,
            "is_deterministic_function": len(non_deterministic_features) == 0,
        },
        "leave_one_triad_out_feature_overlap": {
            "per_fold": triad_feature_overlap,
            "all_folds_100pct_recoverable_by_feature_lookup": all_recoverable,
            "interpretation": (
                "En cada fold, el 100% de los pares de prueba tiene una clave "
                "(fase,pos) ya vista en entrenamiento con la misma etiqueta. "
                "No hay fuga de node_id/triad_index (el modelo no los usa), "
                "pero la función a aprender es trivialmente compartida."
            ),
        },
        "class_counts": dict(counts),
        "implications": [
            "No se requiere arquitectura compleja (GAT) para este corpus: el MLP y el oracle bastan.",
            "Las ablaciones sin fase/posición degradan el rendimiento porque rompen la función determinista.",
            "La validación empírica real del marco exige datos cuyas etiquetas NO se deriven de las mismas reglas (véase datasets/independent_eval/ y external_eval/).",
            "Reportar 100% como 'recuperación de relaciones generadas por reglas' es correcto; reportarlo como 'validación del marco en el mundo real' no lo es.",
        ],
    }

    out = ROOT / "experiments" / "results" / "perfect_metrics_audit.json"
    out.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    out.write_text(json.dumps(report, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")

    print("Auditoría de métricas perfectas")
    print(f"  Gold vs regla determinista: mismatches={len(mismatches)}")
    print(f"  Función determinista fase+pos: {len(non_deterministic_features) == 0}")
    print(f"  LOO recoverable por lookup de features: {all_recoverable}")
    print(f"  Informe: {out}")
    print()
    print(report["conclusion"])
    return 0 if len(mismatches) == 0 and all_recoverable else 1


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())

FILE: scripts/capture_environment.py

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Captura versiones del entorno de reproducción en un artefacto JSON."""
from __future__ import annotations
import importlib.metadata
import json
import platform
import subprocess
from pathlib import Path

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]

def command_version(command: list[str]) -> str | None:
    try:
        result = subprocess.run(command, check=True, capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=20)
        return (result.stdout or result.stderr).strip().splitlines()[0]
    except (OSError, subprocess.SubprocessError):
        return None

def main() -> None:
    packages = {}
    for name in ("rdflib", "pyshacl", "owlrl", "numpy", "torch"):
        try:
            packages[name] = importlib.metadata.version(name)
        except importlib.metadata.PackageNotFoundError:
            packages[name] = None
    data = {
        "python": platform.python_version(),
        "implementation": platform.python_implementation(),
        "platform": platform.platform(),
        "packages": packages,
        "lean_toolchain_file": (ROOT / "lean-toolchain").read_text(encoding="utf-8").strip(),
        "lean_version": command_version(["lean", "--version"]),
        "lake_version": command_version(["lake", "--version"]),
        "requirements": (ROOT / "requirements.txt").read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines(),
    }
    out = ROOT / "experiments" / "results" / "software_versions.json"
    out.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    out.write_text(json.dumps(data, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
    print(out)
    print(json.dumps(data, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2))

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()



================================================
FILE: scripts/check_generated_committed.py
================================================
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Comprueba que los CSV versionados coinciden con generate_ontology.py.

Solo se exige igualdad exacta de:

  - datasets/triad_nodes.csv
  - datasets/vector_steps.csv
  - datasets/relation_candidates.csv
  - datasets/GENERATED_FROM.json  (spec_version del generador)

El TTL no se compara byte-a-byte (serialización rdflib).
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import subprocess
import sys
from pathlib import Path

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]

GENERATED_CSV = [
    "datasets/triad_nodes.csv",
    "datasets/vector_steps.csv",
    "datasets/relation_candidates.csv",
    "datasets/GENERATED_FROM.json",
]


def run(cmd: list[str]) -> subprocess.CompletedProcess:
    return subprocess.run(cmd, cwd=ROOT, capture_output=True, text=True)


def main() -> int:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    parser.add_argument("--with-manifest", action="store_true")
    parser.add_argument("--skip-generate", action="store_true")
    parser.add_argument("--include-ttl", action="store_true")
    args = parser.parse_args()

    if not args.skip_generate:
        print(">>> generate_ontology.py")
        r = run([sys.executable, "scripts/generate_ontology.py"])
        print(r.stdout, end="")
        if r.returncode != 0:
            print(r.stderr, file=sys.stderr)
            return 1

    paths = list(GENERATED_CSV)
    if args.include_ttl:
        paths.insert(0, "ontology/io_ontology.ttl")

    if args.with_manifest:
        print(">>> create_manifest.py")
        r = run([sys.executable, "scripts/create_manifest.py"])
        print(r.stdout, end="")
        if r.returncode != 0:
            print(r.stderr, file=sys.stderr)
            return 1
        paths.append("MANIFEST.sha256")

    print(">>> git diff --ignore-space-at-eol --exit-code", " ".join(paths))
    r = run(["git", "diff", "--ignore-space-at-eol", "--exit-code", "--", *paths])
    if r.returncode != 0:
        print(
            "ERROR: los artefactos generados no coinciden con lo versionado.",
            file=sys.stderr,
        )
        print(r.stdout or r.stderr, file=sys.stderr)
        return 1

    print("OK: derivados (CSV + GENERATED_FROM) coinciden con HEAD")
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())



================================================
FILE: scripts/create_manifest.py
================================================
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Escribe SHA-256 de los artefactos fuente/reproducibles incluidos en el paquete.

Exclusiones (no forman parte del punto fijo del manifiesto):
  - MANIFEST.sha256 — evita hash auto-referente
  - AUDITORIA_AUTO.md — regenerable con timestamp
  - ontology/io_ontology.ttl — regenerable; orden de serialización rdflib no es
    estable byte-a-byte entre corridas (CI lo regenera antes de SHACL)
  - __pycache__, .pytest_cache, .venv

Los CSV canónicos (triad_nodes, vector_steps, relation_candidates) SÍ entran:
son el punto fijo de generate_ontology.py.
"""
from hashlib import sha256
from pathlib import Path

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
INCLUDE_DIRS = ("IOM", "data", "datasets", "docs", "experiments", "ontology", "scripts", "tests")
INCLUDE_FILES = (
    "IOM.lean", "README.md", "AUDITORIA.md", "EXPERIMENTOS.md", "REPRODUCCION.md", "paper.md",
    "MANIFIESTO.md",
    "requirements.txt", "requirements-lock.txt", "lakefile.lean", "lake-manifest.json", "lean-toolchain",
    "LICENSE", ".gitignore", ".github/workflows/ci.yml",
)
EXCLUDE_NAMES = frozenset({
    "MANIFEST.sha256",
    "AUDITORIA_AUTO.md",
    "io_ontology.ttl",  # regenerable; serialización no determinista
})
EXCLUDE_DIR_PARTS = frozenset({"__pycache__", ".pytest_cache", ".venv", "node_modules"})


def main() -> None:
    files = {ROOT / name for name in INCLUDE_FILES if (ROOT / name).is_file()}
    for dirname in INCLUDE_DIRS:
        folder = ROOT / dirname
        if folder.exists():
            files.update(
                p for p in folder.rglob("*")
                if p.is_file()
                and not any(part in EXCLUDE_DIR_PARTS for part in p.parts)
            )
    files = {p for p in files if p.name not in EXCLUDE_NAMES}
    out = ROOT / "MANIFEST.sha256"
    lines = [
        f"{sha256(path.read_bytes()).hexdigest()}  {path.relative_to(ROOT).as_posix()}"
        for path in sorted(files)
    ]
    out.write_text("\n".join(lines) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
    print(f"SHA-256: {len(lines)} archivos -> {out}")
    print(f"  excluidos por nombre: {sorted(EXCLUDE_NAMES)}")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

FILE: scripts/generate_audit_report.py
================================================
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Informe de auditoría regenerable (encuentro). No se versiona como fundamento."""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from pathlib import Path

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]


def load_json(rel: str):
    path = ROOT / rel
    if not path.is_file():
        return None
    return json.loads(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))


def main() -> None:
    out_dir = ROOT / "experiments" / "results"
    out_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    metrics = load_json("experiments/results/pi_hgat_t_metrics.json")
    baselines = load_json("experiments/results/baselines_comparison.json")
    versions = load_json("experiments/results/software_versions.json")

    lines = [
        "# AUDITORIA_AUTO",
        "",
        f"Generado: {datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()}",
        "",
        "> Informe fechado. **No** es prueba de punto fijo ni fundamento del marco.",
        "",
        "## Capas",
        "",
        "- Núcleo: `self_bootstrap.py`, Lean, SHACL, invariancia",
        "- Encuentro: EXP-A/E, PI-HGAT-T/baselines (opcionales)",
        "",
    ]
    if metrics is None:
        lines.append(
            "_No se encontró pi_hgat_t_metrics.json; "
            "ejecutar experiments/optional/pi_hgat_t.py primero._"
        )
    else:
        lines.append("## PI-HGAT-T (diagnóstico)")
        lines.append("```json")
        lines.append(json.dumps(metrics.get("primary_metrics", metrics), indent=2)[:2000])
        lines.append("```")
    if baselines:
        lines.append("## Baselines")
        lines.append("```json")
        lines.append(json.dumps(baselines.get("baselines", baselines), indent=2)[:2000])
        lines.append("```")
    if versions:
        lines.append("## Software")
        lines.append(json.dumps(versions, indent=2))

    out_md = out_dir / "AUDITORIA_AUTO.md"
    out_md.write_text("\n".join(lines) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
    print(f"Escrito {out_md}")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()



================================================
FILE: scripts/generate_ontology.py
================================================
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Genera RDF/OWL y datasets desde data/iom_spec.json, única fuente tabular."""
from __future__ import annotations

import csv
import json
from pathlib import Path
from rdflib import BNode, Graph, Literal, Namespace
from rdflib.namespace import OWL, RDF, RDFS, XSD

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
SPEC = json.loads((ROOT / "data" / "iom_spec.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
IO = Namespace("http://example.org/iom#")
g = Graph()
for prefix, namespace in (("io", IO), ("rdfs", RDFS), ("xsd", XSD), ("owl", OWL)):
    g.bind(prefix, namespace)

ontology = IO.Ontology
g.add((ontology, RDF.type, OWL.Ontology))
g.add((ontology, OWL.versionInfo, Literal(SPEC["spec_version"])))
g.add((ontology, RDFS.label, Literal("Ontología IOM", lang="es")))
g.add((ontology, RDFS.comment, Literal(SPEC["source_note"], lang="es")))
classes = ("OntoNode", "VectorStep", "Perspective", "ExpandedCategory", "Position", "Direction", "SourceModel")
for name in classes:
    g.add((IO[name], RDF.type, OWL.Class))
for names in (("OntoNode", "VectorStep", "ExpandedCategory", "Perspective"),):
    axiom = BNode("iomDisjointCoreClasses")
    head = BNode("iomDisjointClassList0")
    g.add((axiom, RDF.type, OWL.AllDisjointClasses))
    g.add((axiom, OWL.members, head))
    cells = [BNode(f"iomDisjointClassList{i}") for i in range(len(names))]
    for index, name in enumerate(names):
        g.add((cells[index], RDF.first, IO[name]))
        g.add((cells[index], RDF.rest, cells[index + 1] if index + 1 < len(cells) else RDF.nil))

object_properties = {
    "hasPerspective": ("OntoNode", "Perspective"),
    "hasPosition": ("OntoNode", "Position"),
    "hasDirection": ("OntoNode", "Direction"),
    "hasVector": ("VectorStep", "Perspective"),
    "mirrorOf": ("OntoNode", "OntoNode"),
    "creates": ("OntoNode", "OntoNode"),
}
datatype_properties = {
    "hasTriadIndex": ("OntoNode", XSD.integer),
    "hasPhase": ("OntoNode", XSD.string),
    "hasLocalPos": ("OntoNode", XSD.integer),
    "displayLabel": ("OntoNode", XSD.string),
    "hasCyclePhase": ("VectorStep", XSD.string),
    "hasAxisPosition": ("VectorStep", XSD.integer),
    "hasStepIndex": ("VectorStep", XSD.integer),
    "displayArrow": ("VectorStep", XSD.string),
    "hasLevel": ("ExpandedCategory", XSD.integer),
    "declaredTriadCount": ("SourceModel", XSD.integer),
    "declaredPerspectiveCount": ("SourceModel", XSD.integer),
    "declaredOperatorCount": ("SourceModel", XSD.integer),
    "declaredExpandedCategoryCount": ("SourceModel", XSD.integer),
}
for name, (domain, range_) in object_properties.items():
    prop = IO[name]
    g.add((prop, RDF.type, OWL.ObjectProperty))
    g.add((prop, RDFS.domain, IO[domain]))
    g.add((prop, RDFS.range, IO[range_]))
for name, (domain, range_) in datatype_properties.items():
    prop = IO[name]
    g.add((prop, RDF.type, OWL.DatatypeProperty))
    g.add((prop, RDFS.domain, IO[domain]))
    g.add((prop, RDFS.range, range_))
for name in ("hasPerspective", "hasPosition", "hasDirection", "hasVector", "hasTriadIndex", "hasPhase", "hasLocalPos", "displayLabel", "hasCyclePhase", "hasAxisPosition", "hasStepIndex", "displayArrow", "hasLevel"):
    g.add((IO[name], RDF.type, OWL.FunctionalProperty))
g.add((IO.mirrorOf, RDF.type, OWL.SymmetricProperty))

for pos, label in [("→", "→"), ("0", "0"), ("←", "←")]:
    g.add((IO[pos], RDF.type, OWL.NamedIndividual))
    g.add((IO[pos], RDF.type, IO.Position))
    g.add((IO[pos], RDFS.label, Literal(label, lang="es")))
for direction in ("evol", "invol"):
    g.add((IO[direction], RDF.type, OWL.NamedIndividual))
    g.add((IO[direction], RDF.type, IO.Direction))
_perspective_labels = SPEC.get("perspective_labels") or {
    "Ind": "Individualidad", "D": "Dualidad", "Tot": "Totalidad",
    "Evol": "Evolución", "Invol": "Involución",
}
for name, label in _perspective_labels.items():
    g.add((IO[name], RDF.type, IO.Perspective))
    g.add((IO[name], RDFS.label, Literal(label, lang="es")))

position_meta = SPEC["positions"]
position_indices = position_meta["indices"]
position_names_adv = ["→", "0", "→"]
position_names_ret = ["←", "0", "←"]
perspective_names = position_meta["perspectives"]
perspectives = [IO[name] for name in perspective_names]
triad_meta = SPEC["triads"]
triad_rows = {phase: triad_meta[phase] for phase in ("adv", "ret")}
index_base = triad_meta.get("index_base", 0)
n_triads = len(triad_rows["adv"])
triad_ids = range(index_base, index_base + n_triads)
nodes_csv = []
for phase, rows in triad_rows.items():
    direction = IO[SPEC["phases"][phase]["direction"]]
    for row_index, labels in enumerate(rows):
        triad_idx = index_base + row_index
        for pos_idx, label in enumerate(labels):
            node = IO[f"T{triad_idx:02d}_{phase}_P{pos_idx}"]
            g.add((node, RDF.type, IO.OntoNode))
            g.add((node, IO.hasTriadIndex, Literal(triad_idx, datatype=XSD.integer)))
            g.add((node, IO.hasPhase, Literal(phase, datatype=XSD.string)))
            g.add((node, IO.hasLocalPos, Literal(pos_idx, datatype=XSD.integer)))
            display_labels = position_meta["adv_labels" if phase == "adv" else "ret_labels"]
            display_label = display_labels[pos_idx]
            g.add((node, IO.displayLabel, Literal(display_label, datatype=XSD.string)))
            pos_names = position_names_adv if phase == "adv" else position_names_ret
            g.add((node, IO.hasPosition, IO[pos_names[pos_idx]]))
            g.add((node, IO.hasPerspective, perspectives[pos_idx]))
            g.add((node, IO.hasDirection, direction))
            g.add((node, RDFS.label, Literal(label, lang="es")))
            nodes_csv.append({"node_id": str(node).split("#", 1)[1], "triad_index": triad_idx, "phase": phase,
                              "direction": str(direction).split("#", 1)[1], "position_index": pos_idx,
                              "position": pos_names[pos_idx], "display_label": display_label,
                              "perspective": perspective_names[pos_idx], "label": label})

for triad_idx in triad_ids:
    for pos_idx in range(3):
        adv = IO[f"T{triad_idx:02d}_adv_P{pos_idx}"]
        ret = IO[f"T{triad_idx:02d}_ret_P{2-pos_idx}"]
        g.add((adv, IO.mirrorOf, ret))
        g.add((ret, IO.mirrorOf, adv))

for triad_idx in triad_ids:
    for rule in SPEC["creation_rules"]:
        for source_pos in rule["source_positions"]:
            for target_pos in rule["target_positions"]:
                source = IO[f"T{triad_idx:02d}_{rule['source_phase']}_P{source_pos}"]
                target = IO[f"T{triad_idx:02d}_{rule['target_phase']}_P{target_pos}"]
                g.add((source, IO.creates, target))

vector_csv = []
for vector_row in SPEC["vectors"]:
    vector, phase = vector_row["name"], vector_row["phase"]
    for step_index, (label, axis_pos) in enumerate(zip(vector_row["labels"], vector_row["axis_positions"])):
        step = IO[f"vector_{vector.lower()}_{phase}_{step_index}"]
        g.add((step, RDF.type, IO.VectorStep))
        g.add((step, IO.hasVector, IO[vector]))
        g.add((step, IO.hasCyclePhase, Literal(phase, datatype=XSD.string)))
        g.add((step, IO.hasAxisPosition, Literal(axis_pos, datatype=XSD.integer)))
        g.add((step, IO.hasStepIndex, Literal(step_index, datatype=XSD.integer)))
        g.add((step, IO.displayArrow, Literal(vector_row["arrow"], datatype=XSD.string)))
        g.add((step, RDFS.label, Literal(label, lang="es")))
        vector_csv.append({"step_id": str(step).split("#", 1)[1], "vector": vector, "phase": phase,
                           "step_index": step_index, "axis_position": axis_pos, "label": label,
                           "arrow": vector_row["arrow"]})

for level, labels in SPEC["expanded_categories"].items():
    for index, label in enumerate(labels, 1):
        category = IO[f"expanded_L{level}_{index:02d}"]
        g.add((category, RDF.type, IO.ExpandedCategory))
        g.add((category, IO.hasLevel, Literal(int(level), datatype=XSD.integer)))
        g.add((category, RDFS.label, Literal(label, lang="es")))

model = IO.SourceModel
g.add((model, RDF.type, IO.SourceModel))
for prop, value in ((IO.declaredTriadCount, 13), (IO.declaredPerspectiveCount, 5),
                    (IO.declaredOperatorCount, 4), (IO.declaredExpandedCategoryCount, 21)):
    g.add((model, prop, Literal(value, datatype=XSD.integer)))

relation_csv = []
relation_lookup = {}
for subject, _, obj in g.triples((None, IO.mirrorOf, None)):
    relation_lookup[(str(subject), str(obj))] = "mirrorOf"
for subject, _, obj in g.triples((None, IO.creates, None)):
    relation_lookup[(str(subject), str(obj))] = "creates"
for triad_idx in triad_ids:
    ids = [IO[f"T{triad_idx:02d}_{phase}_P{pos}"] for phase in ("adv", "ret") for pos in range(3)]
    for source in ids:
        for target in ids:
            if source == target:
                continue
            relation_csv.append({"triad_index": triad_idx, "source": str(source).split("#", 1)[1],
                                 "target": str(target).split("#", 1)[1],
                                 "relation": relation_lookup.get((str(source), str(target)), "none")})

assert len(triad_rows["adv"]) == len(triad_rows["ret"]) == 13
assert position_indices == [0, 1, 2]
assert len(nodes_csv) == 78
assert len(vector_csv) == 20
assert sum(1 for _ in g.triples((None, IO.creates, None))) == 104
assert len(relation_csv) == 13 * 6 * 5

out = ROOT / "ontology" / "io_ontology.ttl"
out.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
out.write_text(g.serialize(format="turtle").rstrip() + "\n", encoding="utf-8")

def write_csv(path: Path, rows: list[dict]) -> None:
    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    with path.open("w", newline="", encoding="utf-8") as handle:
        writer = csv.DictWriter(handle, fieldnames=list(rows[0]), lineterminator="\n")
        writer.writeheader()
        writer.writerows(rows)

write_csv(ROOT / "datasets" / "triad_nodes.csv", nodes_csv)
write_csv(ROOT / "datasets" / "vector_steps.csv", vector_csv)
write_csv(ROOT / "datasets" / "relation_candidates.csv", relation_csv)

_stamp = {
    "spec_version": SPEC["spec_version"],
    "schema_version": SPEC.get("schema_version"),
    "source_date": SPEC.get("source_date"),
    "purpose_id": (SPEC.get("purpose") or {}).get("id"),
    "purpose_label": (SPEC.get("purpose") or {}).get("label"),
    "artifacts": [
        "datasets/triad_nodes.csv",
        "datasets/vector_steps.csv",
        "datasets/relation_candidates.csv",
        "ontology/io_ontology.ttl",
    ],
}
(ROOT / "datasets" / "GENERATED_FROM.json").write_text(
    json.dumps(_stamp, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2) + "\n", encoding="utf-8"
)
print(f"Tríadas: {n_triads} por fase (índice {index_base}–{index_base + n_triads - 1}); nodos: {len(nodes_csv)}; creates: 104; espejos: 78")
print(f"Pasos vectoriales: {len(vector_csv)}; candidatos PI-HGAT-T: {len(relation_csv)}")
print(f"OWL/Turtle: {out}")
print(f"Sello: datasets/GENERATED_FROM.json spec_version={SPEC['spec_version']}")



================================================
FILE: scripts/importar_datos_personalizados.py
================================================
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Plantilla de ingesta guiada: alinea un CSV de conceptos externos a la ontología IOM de 78 nodos.

No modifica data/iom_spec.json ni la ontología core. Valida que los mapped_node_id
existen, comprueba consistencia de perspectiva/fase/posición y genera un CSV alineado
más un informe de validación.

Uso:
  python scripts/importar_datos_personalizados.py \
      --input datasets/ingestion/templates/biologia_depredador_presa.csv \
      --output datasets/ingestion/aligned/biologia_aligned.csv

Opciones:
  --input PATH     CSV de entrada (obligatorio)
  --output PATH    CSV alineado de salida (por defecto: datasets/ingestion/aligned/<stem>_aligned.csv)
  --strict         Fallar si hay cualquier warning (por defecto solo error en mapped_node_id inválido)
  --report PATH    Ruta del informe JSON (por defecto junto al output)
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import csv
import json
import sys
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
NODES_CSV = ROOT / "datasets" / "triad_nodes.csv"
VALID_PERSPECTIVES = {"Ind", "D", "Tot", "Evol", "Invol"}
VALID_PHASES = {"adv", "ret"}
VALID_POSITIONS = {"left", "center", "right"}
VALID_RELATIONS = {"none", "mirrorOf", "creates"}
REQUIRED_COLUMNS = {"external_id", "external_label"}


def load_canonical_nodes() -> dict[str, dict[str, Any]]:
    """Carga los 78 nodos canónicos indexados por node_id."""
    if not NODES_CSV.is_file():
        print(
            f"ERROR: no se encuentra {NODES_CSV}. "
            "Ejecuta primero: python scripts/generate_ontology.py",
            file=sys.stderr,
        )
        raise SystemExit(1)
    nodes: dict[str, dict[str, Any]] = {}
    with NODES_CSV.open(encoding="utf-8", newline="") as fh:
        reader = csv.DictReader(fh)
        for row in reader:
            nodes[row["node_id"]] = row
    if len(nodes) != 78:
        print(
            f"ADVERTENCIA: se esperaban 78 nodos, se cargaron {len(nodes)}. "
            "Regenera los datasets con generate_ontology.py.",
            file=sys.stderr,
        )
    return nodes


def read_input_csv(path: Path) -> list[dict[str, str]]:
    """Lee el CSV de entrada ignorando líneas de comentario que empiezan por #."""
    rows: list[dict[str, str]] = []
    with path.open(encoding="utf-8", newline="") as fh:
        # Filtrar comentarios antes de pasar al DictReader
        lines = [ln for ln in fh if not ln.strip().startswith("#")]
    from io import StringIO

    reader = csv.DictReader(StringIO("".join(lines)))
    if reader.fieldnames is None:
        print("ERROR: el CSV no tiene cabecera.", file=sys.stderr)
        raise SystemExit(1)
    missing = REQUIRED_COLUMNS - set(reader.fieldnames)
    if missing:
        print(f"ERROR: faltan columnas obligatorias: {sorted(missing)}", file=sys.stderr)
        raise SystemExit(1)
    for row in reader:
        # Normalizar claves
        cleaned = {k.strip(): (v.strip() if isinstance(v, str) else v) for k, v in row.items()}
        if not cleaned.get("external_id"):
            continue
        rows.append(cleaned)
    return rows


def validate_row(
    row: dict[str, str],
    nodes: dict[str, dict[str, Any]],
    row_num: int,
) -> tuple[dict[str, Any], list[str], list[str]]:
    """Valida una fila y devuelve (fila_alineada, errores, warnings)."""
    errors: list[str] = []
    warnings: list[str] = []
    aligned: dict[str, Any] = {
        "external_id": row["external_id"],
        "external_label": row.get("external_label", ""),
        "mapped_node_id": row.get("mapped_node_id", ""),
        "perspective": row.get("perspective", ""),
        "phase": row.get("phase", ""),
        "position": row.get("position", ""),
        "relation_candidate": row.get("relation_candidate", "none") or "none",
        "notes": row.get("notes", ""),
        "canonical_label": "",
        "canonical_perspective": "",
        "canonical_phase": "",
        "canonical_position": "",
        "status": "ok",
    }

    node_id = aligned["mapped_node_id"]
    if not node_id:
        errors.append(f"fila {row_num}: mapped_node_id vacío")
        aligned["status"] = "error"
        return aligned, errors, warnings

    if node_id not in nodes:
        errors.append(
            f"fila {row_num}: mapped_node_id '{node_id}' no existe en los 78 nodos canónicos"
        )
        aligned["status"] = "error"
        return aligned, errors, warnings

    canon = nodes[node_id]
    aligned["canonical_label"] = canon.get("label", "")
    aligned["canonical_perspective"] = canon.get("perspective", "")
    aligned["canonical_phase"] = canon.get("phase", "")
    aligned["canonical_position"] = canon.get("position", "")

    # Consistencia opcional de perspectiva / fase / posición
    if aligned["perspective"] and aligned["perspective"] not in VALID_PERSPECTIVES:
        warnings.append(
            f"fila {row_num}: perspective '{aligned['perspective']}' no es canónica "
            f"({sorted(VALID_PERSPECTIVES)})"
        )
    elif aligned["perspective"] and aligned["perspective"] != canon.get("perspective"):
        # Solo advertir si el usuario forzó un valor distinto del nodo
        if aligned["perspective"] in {"Ind", "D", "Tot"}:
            warnings.append(
                f"fila {row_num}: perspective declarada '{aligned['perspective']}' "
                f"≠ perspectiva del nodo '{canon.get('perspective')}'"
            )

    if aligned["phase"] and aligned["phase"] not in VALID_PHASES:
        warnings.append(
            f"fila {row_num}: phase '{aligned['phase']}' no es canónica ({sorted(VALID_PHASES)})"
        )
    elif aligned["phase"] and aligned["phase"] != canon.get("phase"):
        warnings.append(
            f"fila {row_num}: phase declarada '{aligned['phase']}' ≠ fase del nodo '{canon.get('phase')}'"
        )

    if aligned["position"] and aligned["position"] not in VALID_POSITIONS:
        warnings.append(
            f"fila {row_num}: position '{aligned['position']}' no es canónica "
            f"({sorted(VALID_POSITIONS)})"
        )
    elif aligned["position"] and aligned["position"] != canon.get("position"):
        warnings.append(
            f"fila {row_num}: position declarada '{aligned['position']}' "
            f"≠ posición del nodo '{canon.get('position')}'"
        )

    if aligned["relation_candidate"] not in VALID_RELATIONS:
        warnings.append(
            f"fila {row_num}: relation_candidate '{aligned['relation_candidate']}' "
            f"no es válida ({sorted(VALID_RELATIONS)}); se fuerza a 'none'"
        )
        aligned["relation_candidate"] = "none"

    if warnings and aligned["status"] == "ok":
        aligned["status"] = "warning"

    return aligned, errors, warnings


def main() -> int:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(
        description="Alinea un CSV de conceptos externos a la ontología IOM de 78 nodos."
    )
    parser.add_argument("--input", "-i", required=True, type=Path, help="CSV de entrada")
    parser.add_argument(
        "--output",
        "-o",
        type=Path,
        default=None,
        help="CSV alineado de salida",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--strict",
        action="store_true",
        help="Tratar warnings como errores (exit code 1)",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--report",
        type=Path,
        default=None,
        help="Ruta del informe JSON de validación",
    )
    args = parser.parse_args()

    if not args.input.is_file():
        print(f"ERROR: no existe el archivo de entrada: {args.input}", file=sys.stderr)
        return 1

    nodes = load_canonical_nodes()
    rows = read_input_csv(args.input)
    if not rows:
        print("ERROR: el CSV de entrada no contiene filas de datos.", file=sys.stderr)
        return 1

    aligned_rows: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    all_errors: list[str] = []
    all_warnings: list[str] = []

    for i, row in enumerate(rows, start=2):  # +2 por cabecera y 1-index
        aligned, errors, warnings = validate_row(row, nodes, i)
        aligned_rows.append(aligned)
        all_errors.extend(errors)
        all_warnings.extend(warnings)

    # Determinar rutas de salida
    if args.output is None:
        out_dir = ROOT / "datasets" / "ingestion" / "aligned"
        out_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        args.output = out_dir / f"{args.input.stem}_aligned.csv"
    else:
        args.output.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

    if args.report is None:
        args.report = args.output.with_suffix(".report.json")

    # Escribir CSV alineado
    fieldnames = [
        "external_id",
        "external_label",
        "mapped_node_id",
        "canonical_label",
        "canonical_perspective",
        "canonical_phase",
        "canonical_position",
        "perspective",
        "phase",
        "position",
        "relation_candidate",
        "status",
        "notes",
    ]
    with args.output.open("w", encoding="utf-8", newline="") as fh:
        writer = csv.DictWriter(fh, fieldnames=fieldnames, extrasaction="ignore")
        writer.writeheader()
        writer.writerows(aligned_rows)

    report = {
        "input": str(args.input),
        "output": str(args.output),
        "canonical_nodes": len(nodes),
        "rows_processed": len(aligned_rows),
        "errors": all_errors,
        "warnings": all_warnings,
        "ok_count": sum(1 for r in aligned_rows if r["status"] == "ok"),
        "warning_count": sum(1 for r in aligned_rows if r["status"] == "warning"),
        "error_count": sum(1 for r in aligned_rows if r["status"] == "error"),
    }
    args.report.write_text(json.dumps(report, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")

    # Resumen en consola
    print(f"Entrada:   {args.input}")
    print(f"Salida:    {args.output}")
    print(f"Informe:   {args.report}")
    print(f"Filas:     {report['rows_processed']}  "
          f"(ok={report['ok_count']}, warning={report['warning_count']}, error={report['error_count']})")
    if all_errors:
        print("\nERRORES:")
        for e in all_errors:
            print(f"  - {e}")
    if all_warnings:
        print("\nADVERTENCIAS:")
        for w in all_warnings:
            print(f"  - {w}")

    if all_errors:
        print("\nResultado: FALLO (hay errores de mapeo)")
        return 1
    if args.strict and all_warnings:
        print("\nResultado: FALLO (--strict y hay advertencias)")
        return 1
    print("\nResultado: OK — los conceptos se alinearon a la ontología canónica sin romper la bóveda.")
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())

def main() -> None:
    data = zlib.decompress(base64.b64decode(PAYLOAD))
    digest = hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest()
    if digest != EXPECTED:
        raise SystemExit(f"hash mismatch: {digest} != {EXPECTED}")
    OUT.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    OUT.write_bytes(data)
    print(f"OK {OUT} ({len(data)} bytes) sha256={digest}")

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

FILE: scripts/measure_invariance.py
================================================
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Invariancia interna no trivial (con control negativo).

Mide autonomía *y* distingue el grafo canónico de uno corrompido:

1. Auto-regeneración: etiqueta ≡ regla (fase, pos) aplicada al par.
2. Invariancia bajo índice de tríada.
3. Reconstrucción desde subconjunto (LOO feature lookup sin etiquetas del held-out).
4. **Control negativo:** al corromper una arista del grafo, (1) o (3) debe fallar.

Sin el control negativo, un 1.0 podría ser tautología.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import copy
import csv
import json
from collections import defaultdict
from pathlib import Path

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]


def parse_node(node_id: str) -> tuple[str, int]:
    parts = node_id.split("_")
    return parts[1], int(parts[2][1:])


def rule_label(src: str, tgt: str) -> str:
    sp, spos = parse_node(src)
    tp, tpos = parse_node(tgt)
    if sp == tp:
        return "none"
    if tpos == 2 - spos:
        return "mirrorOf"
    if (spos in (0, 2) and tpos == 1) or (spos == 1 and tpos in (0, 2)):
        return "creates"
    return "none"


def feature_key(src: str, tgt: str) -> tuple:
    sp, spos = parse_node(src)
    tp, tpos = parse_node(tgt)
    return (sp, spos, tp, tpos)


def self_regen_rate(rows: list[dict]) -> float:
    mism = sum(1 for r in rows if rule_label(r["source"], r["target"]) != r["relation"])
    return 1.0 - mism / max(len(rows), 1)


def triad_invariant(rows: list[dict]) -> bool:
    by_feat: dict[tuple, set[str]] = defaultdict(set)
    for r in rows:
        by_feat[feature_key(r["source"], r["target"])].add(r["relation"])
    return all(len(v) == 1 for v in by_feat.values())


def subset_reconstruction(rows: list[dict]) -> bool:
    for held in range(1, 14):
        train: dict[tuple, str] = {}
        test: list[tuple[tuple, str]] = []
        for r in rows:
            key = feature_key(r["source"], r["target"])
            tid = int(r["triad_index"])
            if tid == held:
                test.append((key, r["relation"]))
            else:
                train[key] = r["relation"]
        if any(train.get(k) != lab for k, lab in test):
            return False
    return True


def corrupt_one_edge(rows: list[dict]) -> list[dict]:
    """Cambia la etiqueta de la primera arista mirrorOf a none (grafo inválido)."""
    out = copy.deepcopy(rows)
    for r in out:
        if r["relation"] == "mirrorOf":
            r["relation"] = "none"
            break
    return out


def main() -> int:
    rows = list(
        csv.DictReader(
            (ROOT / "datasets" / "relation_candidates.csv").open(
                encoding="utf-8", newline=""
            )
        )
    )

    regen = self_regen_rate(rows)
    inv = triad_invariant(rows)
    subset = subset_reconstruction(rows)

    corrupted = corrupt_one_edge(rows)
    # Tras corrupción: o bien auto-regeneración cae, o el grafo ya no coincide con la regla
    corrupted_regen = self_regen_rate(corrupted)
    negative_control_detects = corrupted_regen < 1.0

    autonomy = regen == 1.0 and inv and subset
    non_trivial = autonomy and negative_control_detects

    report = {
        "framing": (
            "Invariancia no trivial: el canónico regenera 1.0; un grafo con una "
            "arista corrupta debe fallar el control negativo."
        ),
        "canonical": {
            "self_regeneration_rate": round(regen, 6),
            "triad_index_invariance": inv,
            "subset_reconstruction": subset,
            "autonomy_measurable": autonomy,
        },
        "negative_control": {
            "description": "primera arista mirrorOf relabelada a none",
            "corrupted_self_regeneration_rate": round(corrupted_regen, 6),
            "detects_corruption": negative_control_detects,
            "interpretation": (
                "si detects_corruption es false, la métrica no distingue "
                "grafo correcto de corrompido (tautología)"
            ),
        },
        "invariance_non_trivial": non_trivial,
    }

    out = ROOT / "experiments" / "results" / "invariance_metrics.json"
    out.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    out.write_text(json.dumps(report, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2, sort_keys=True) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")

    print("Invariancia interna (con control negativo)")
    print(f"  Auto-regeneración canónica: {regen}")
    print(f"  Invariancia bajo índice de tríada: {inv}")
    print(f"  Reconstrucción desde subconjunto: {subset}")
    print(f"  Control negativo detecta corrupción: {negative_control_detects} (rate={corrupted_regen:.6f})")
    print(f"  Invariancia no trivial: {non_trivial}")
    print(f"  Informe: {out}")
    return 0 if non_trivial else 1


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())



================================================
FILE: scripts/migrate_spec.py
================================================
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Aplica migraciones declarativas a data/iom_spec.json.

Las migraciones se registran en el campo `migrations` del propio JSON.
Este script solo registra y valida; las transformaciones semánticas futuras
deben implementarse como funciones nombradas en MIGRATION_HANDLERS.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import json
from pathlib import Path

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
SPEC_PATH = ROOT / "data" / "iom_spec.json"

# Handlers de migración (from_version -> función). Vacío en 1.1.0 porque el
# cambio fue solo metadatos.
MIGRATION_HANDLERS: dict[str, callable] = {}


def load() -> dict:
    return json.loads(SPEC_PATH.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))


def save(data: dict) -> None:
    SPEC_PATH.write_text(json.dumps(data, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")


def list_migrations(data: dict) -> None:
    migrations = data.get("migrations") or []
    if not migrations:
        print("No hay migraciones registradas.")
        return
    for m in migrations:
        print(f"  {m['from_version']} -> {m['to_version']}: {m['description']} ({m.get('date', '?')})")


def main() -> None:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Gestión de migraciones de iom_spec.json")
    parser.add_argument("--list", action="store_true", help="Listar migraciones registradas")
    parser.add_argument("--check", action="store_true", help="Verificar que spec_version es coherente")
    args = parser.parse_args()

    data = load()
    if args.list:
        print(f"spec_version actual: {data.get('spec_version')}")
        list_migrations(data)
        return
    if args.check:
        version = data.get("spec_version")
        migrations = data.get("migrations") or []
        if migrations:
            last = migrations[-1]["to_version"]
            if last != version:
                raise SystemExit(f"Inconsistencia: último to_version={last} pero spec_version={version}")
        print(f"OK: spec_version={version} coherente con historial de migraciones")
        return
    parser.print_help()


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
