#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Empaqueta los artefactos reproducibles, excluyendo cachés y el checkout Git."""
from pathlib import Path
from zipfile import ZIP_DEFLATED, ZipFile

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
INCLUDE = [
    "README.md", "AUDITORIA.md", "EXPERIMENTOS.md", "REPRODUCCION.md", "paper.md", "LICENSE", "MANIFEST.sha256",
    "IOM.lean", "IOM", "lakefile.lean", "lake-manifest.json", "lean-toolchain", "requirements.txt", "requirements-lock.txt",
    "data", "datasets", "experiments/config.json", "experiments/optional", "experiments/results",
    "experiments/logs", "ontology", "scripts", "tests", ".github/workflows/ci.yml",
]

def files_in(path: Path):
    if path.is_file():
        yield path
    elif path.is_dir():
        for p in path.rglob("*"):
            if p.is_file() and ".git" not in p.parts:
                yield p

def main() -> None:
    out = ROOT / "iom-reproducible.zip"
    with ZipFile(out, "w", ZIP_DEFLATED) as zf:
        for rel in INCLUDE:
            for p in files_in(ROOT / rel):
                zf.write(p, p.relative_to(ROOT).as_posix())
    print(f"Escrito {out}")

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()



================================================
FILE: scripts/score_independent_eval.py
================================================
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Scoring post-hoc de evaluación independiente (capa C).

Formato oficial de anotaciones (annotations_template.json):

    {
      "annotators": ["A1", "A2", "A3"],
      "status": "pending_human_collection" | "complete",
      "items": [
        {
          "id": "NR01",
          "annotations": {"A1": "none" | null, ...},
          "free_text": {"A1": "...", ...}
        },
        ...
      ]
    }

Reglas estadísticas:
  - null / ausente ≠ etiqueta "unsure".
  - Fleiss' kappa exige el **mismo conjunto de anotadores** con respuesta
    no nula en **todos** los ítems incluidos (k fijo). No se trunca.
  - Si un ítem tiene un subconjunto distinto de respondentes, se excluye
    del kappa y se reporta en incomplete/heterogeneous.
  - "unsure" es una respuesta legítima, no un relleno.

Uso:
    python scripts/score_independent_eval.py
    python scripts/score_independent_eval.py --annotations path/to/filled.json
    python scripts/score_independent_eval.py --min-annotators 3
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import json
import sys
from collections import Counter, defaultdict
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
EVAL_DIR = ROOT / "datasets" / "independent_eval"
VALID_LABELS = frozenset({"none", "mirrorOf", "creates", "unsure"})


def fleiss_kappa(ratings: list[list[str]], categories: list[str]) -> float:
    """Fleiss' kappa. ratings[i] = etiquetas de los k anotadores para el ítem i.
    Todas las filas deben tener la misma longitud k ≥ 2.
    """
    n_items = len(ratings)
    if n_items == 0:
        return float("nan")
    n_raters = len(ratings[0])
    if n_raters < 2:
        return float("nan")
    if any(len(r) != n_raters for r in ratings):
        raise ValueError("Todas las filas de ratings deben tener la misma longitud")

    cat_idx = {c: i for i, c in enumerate(categories)}
    table = []
    for row in ratings:
        counts = [0] * len(categories)
        for lab in row:
            if lab not in cat_idx:
                raise ValueError(f"Etiqueta fuera de categorías: {lab!r}")
            counts[cat_idx[lab]] += 1
        table.append(counts)

    P = []
    for counts in table:
        s = sum(c * (c - 1) for c in counts)
        P.append(s / (n_raters * (n_raters - 1)))
    P_bar = sum(P) / n_items

    totals = [sum(table[i][j] for i in range(n_items)) for j in range(len(categories))]
    p = [t / (n_items * n_raters) for t in totals]
    P_e = sum(pj ** 2 for pj in p)
    if abs(1 - P_e) < 1e-12:
        return 1.0
    return (P_bar - P_e) / (1 - P_e)


def parse_template_format(data: dict) -> tuple[list[str], dict[str, dict[str, str | None]]]:
    """Formato oficial: annotators=[ids], items=[{id, annotations: {A: label|null}}]."""
    annotator_ids = list(data.get("annotators") or [])
    if not annotator_ids:
        raise ValueError("Campo 'annotators' vacío o ausente")

    by_pair: dict[str, dict[str, str | None]] = {}
    items = data.get("items")
    if not isinstance(items, list):
        raise ValueError("Campo 'items' debe ser una lista")

    for item in items:
        pid = item.get("id")
        if not pid:
            raise ValueError(f"Ítem sin id: {item!r}")
        anns = item.get("annotations") or {}
        if not isinstance(anns, dict):
            raise ValueError(f"annotations de {pid} debe ser un objeto")
        pair_map: dict[str, str | None] = {}
        for aid in annotator_ids:
            raw = anns.get(aid)
            if raw is None:
                pair_map[aid] = None
            elif isinstance(raw, str) and raw.strip() == "":
                pair_map[aid] = None
            elif raw in VALID_LABELS:
                pair_map[aid] = raw
            else:
                raise ValueError(
                    f"Etiqueta inválida en {pid}/{aid}: {raw!r}. "
                    f"Permitidas: {sorted(VALID_LABELS)} o null"
                )
        by_pair[pid] = pair_map
    return annotator_ids, by_pair


def parse_legacy_formats(data: Any) -> tuple[list[str], dict[str, dict[str, str | None]]]:
    by_pair: dict[str, dict[str, str | None]] = defaultdict(dict)
    annotator_ids: list[str] = []

    if isinstance(data, dict) and "annotators" in data:
        anns = data["annotators"]
        if isinstance(anns, list) and anns and isinstance(anns[0], dict) and "labels" in anns[0]:
            for ann in anns:
                aid = str(ann.get("id") or ann.get("name") or f"A{len(annotator_ids)+1}")
                annotator_ids.append(aid)
                for pair_id, label in (ann.get("labels") or {}).items():
                    by_pair[pair_id][aid] = label if label in VALID_LABELS else None
            return annotator_ids, dict(by_pair)

    if isinstance(data, list):
        aids_set: set[str] = set()
        for item in data:
            pid = item.get("id") or item.get("pair_id")
            aid = str(item.get("annotator") or "A1")
            lab = item.get("label") or item.get("annotation")
            if not pid:
                continue
            aids_set.add(aid)
            by_pair[pid][aid] = lab if lab in VALID_LABELS else None
        return sorted(aids_set), dict(by_pair)

    raise ValueError(
        "Formato de anotaciones no reconocido. "
        "Usa el formato de annotations_template.json."
    )


def load_annotations(path: Path) -> tuple[list[str], dict[str, dict[str, str | None]], dict]:
    data = json.loads(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    meta = {
        "status": data.get("status") if isinstance(data, dict) else None,
        "description": data.get("description") if isinstance(data, dict) else None,
        "evaluation_mode": data.get("evaluation_mode") if isinstance(data, dict) else None,
    }
    if isinstance(data, dict) and isinstance(data.get("items"), list):
        annotator_ids, by_pair = parse_template_format(data)
    else:
        annotator_ids, by_pair = parse_legacy_formats(data)
    return annotator_ids, by_pair, meta


def load_rule_reference() -> dict[str, str]:
    ref_path = EVAL_DIR / "rule_reference.json"
    if not ref_path.is_file():
        return {}
    raw = json.loads(ref_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    if isinstance(raw, list):
        return {item["id"]: item.get("rule_label") for item in raw if "id" in item}
    if isinstance(raw, dict):
        out = {}
        for k, v in raw.items():
            if k in ("labels",):
                continue
            if isinstance(v, dict):
                out[k] = v.get("rule_label")
            elif isinstance(v, str):
                out[k] = v
        if "labels" in raw and isinstance(raw["labels"], dict):
            out.update(raw["labels"])
        return out
    return {}


def main() -> int:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(
        description="Scoring de evaluación independiente (capa C)"
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--annotations",
        type=Path,
        default=EVAL_DIR / "annotations.json",
        help="JSON de anotaciones (plantilla o rellenado)",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--min-annotators",
        type=int,
        default=3,
        help="Número exacto de anotadores con respuesta no nula requerido por ítem (default 3)",
    )
    args = parser.parse_args()

    if not args.annotations.is_file():
        # fallback a plantilla
        alt = EVAL_DIR / "annotations_template.json"
        if alt.is_file():
            args.annotations = alt
        else:
            print(f"ERROR: no se encontró {args.annotations}", file=sys.stderr)
            return 1

    try:
        annotator_ids, by_pair, meta = load_annotations(args.annotations)
    except (ValueError, json.JSONDecodeError) as exc:
        print(f"ERROR de formato: {exc}", file=sys.stderr)
        return 1

    k_required = args.min_annotators
    if k_required < 2:
        print("ERROR: --min-annotators debe ser ≥ 2", file=sys.stderr)
        return 1

    incomplete_pairs: dict[str, dict] = {}
    respondent_sets: dict[str, tuple[str, ...]] = {}
    labels_by_pair_ordered: dict[str, list[str]] = {}
    unsure_counts: Counter = Counter()
    present_counts: Counter = Counter()

    for pid, ann_map in by_pair.items():
        respondents = []
        labels_ordered = []
        missing = []
        for aid in annotator_ids:
            lab = ann_map.get(aid)
            if lab is None:
                missing.append(aid)
            else:
                respondents.append(aid)
                labels_ordered.append(lab)
                present_counts[lab] += 1
                if lab == "unsure":
                    unsure_counts[pid] += 1

        if len(respondents) == k_required:
            respondent_sets[pid] = tuple(respondents)
            labels_by_pair_ordered[pid] = labels_ordered
        else:
            incomplete_pairs[pid] = {
                "n_present": len(respondents),
                "n_missing": len(missing),
                "missing_annotators": missing,
                "present_annotators": respondents,
                "present_labels": labels_ordered,
                "reason": (
                    f"se requieren exactamente {k_required} respuestas no nulas; "
                    f"hay {len(respondents)}"
                ),
            }

    status = meta.get("status") or "unknown"
    n_total = len(by_pair)

    by_respondent_set: dict[tuple[str, ...], list[str]] = defaultdict(list)
    for pid, rset in respondent_sets.items():
        by_respondent_set[rset].append(pid)

    kappa_eligible_ids: list[str] = []
    kappa_annotators: tuple[str, ...] | None = None
    heterogeneous: dict[str, dict] = {}

    if by_respondent_set:
        best_set = max(
            by_respondent_set.keys(),
            key=lambda s: (len(by_respondent_set[s]), s),
        )
        kappa_eligible_ids = sorted(by_respondent_set[best_set])
        kappa_annotators = best_set

        for rset, pids in by_respondent_set.items():
            if rset == best_set:
                continue
            for pid in pids:
                heterogeneous[pid] = {
                    "respondents": list(rset),
                    "n_present": len(rset),
                    "reason": (
                        "conjunto de anotadores distinto al usado para kappa "
                        f"({list(best_set)}); no se trunca ni se mezcla"
                    ),
                    "present_labels": labels_by_pair_ordered[pid],
                }

    n_kappa = len(kappa_eligible_ids)
    n_incomplete = len(incomplete_pairs)
    n_heterogeneous = len(heterogeneous)

    if n_kappa == 0:
        report = {
            "status": status,
            "evaluation_mode": meta.get("evaluation_mode"),
            "n_pairs_total": n_total,
            "n_pairs_in_kappa": 0,
            "n_pairs_incomplete": n_incomplete,
            "n_pairs_heterogeneous_respondent_set": n_heterogeneous,
            "min_annotators_required": k_required,
            "kappa_annotators": None,
            "fleiss_kappa": None,
            "majority_labels": {},
            "posthoc_agreement_with_rule_reference": None,
            "incomplete_detail": incomplete_pairs,
            "heterogeneous_detail": heterogeneous,
            "note": (
                f"Sin ítems con exactamente {k_required} respuestas no nulas "
                "del mismo conjunto de anotadores. No se trunca. "
                "null/ausente ≠ 'unsure'. Protocolo listo; esperando recolección."
            ),
        }
        out = ROOT / "experiments" / "results" / "independent_eval_scores.json"
        out.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        out.write_text(json.dumps(report, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
        print(json.dumps(report, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2))
        print(f"Escrito: {out}")
        return 0

    assert kappa_annotators is not None
    k = len(kappa_annotators)
    categories = ["none", "mirrorOf", "creates", "unsure"]
    ratings = [labels_by_pair_ordered[pid] for pid in kappa_eligible_ids]

    if any(len(r) != k for r in ratings):
        print(
            "ERROR interno: longitudes inconsistentes tras filtrado; no se calcula kappa.",
            file=sys.stderr,
        )
        return 1

    kappa = fleiss_kappa(ratings, categories)

    majority: dict[str, str] = {}
    unanimous = 0
    for pid in kappa_eligible_ids:
        labs = labels_by_pair_ordered[pid]
        cnt = Counter(labs)
        maj_lab, maj_n = cnt.most_common(1)[0]
        majority[pid] = maj_lab
        if maj_n == len(labs):
            unanimous += 1

    majority_all = dict(majority)
    for pid, labs in labels_by_pair_ordered.items():
        if pid not in majority_all and labs:
            majority_all[pid] = Counter(labs).most_common(1)[0][0]

    rule_ref = load_rule_reference()
    comparable = {
        pid: maj
        for pid, maj in majority.items()
        if rule_ref.get(pid) and rule_ref[pid] not in (None, "unspecified")
    }
    agreement_with_rules = None
    if comparable:
        matched = sum(1 for pid, maj in comparable.items() if rule_ref[pid] == maj)
        agreement_with_rules = round(matched / len(comparable), 4)

    report = {
        "status": status,
        "evaluation_mode": meta.get("evaluation_mode"),
        "n_pairs_total": n_total,
        "n_pairs_in_kappa": n_kappa,
        "n_pairs_incomplete": n_incomplete,
        "n_pairs_heterogeneous_respondent_set": n_heterogeneous,
        "min_annotators_required": k_required,
        "kappa_annotators": list(kappa_annotators),
        "n_annotators_per_kappa_item": k,
        "fleiss_kappa": None if (isinstance(kappa, float) and kappa != kappa) else round(kappa, 4),
        "unanimous_fraction": round(unanimous / n_kappa, 4) if n_kappa else None,
        "majority_labels_in_kappa": majority,
        "majority_labels_all_with_any_response": majority_all,
        "label_distribution_present": dict(present_counts),
        "unsure_count_by_pair": dict(unsure_counts),
        "posthoc_agreement_with_rule_reference": agreement_with_rules,
        "n_posthoc_comparable": len(comparable),
        "incomplete_detail": incomplete_pairs if incomplete_pairs else {},
        "heterogeneous_detail": heterogeneous if heterogeneous else {},
        "policy": (
            "Fleiss' kappa solo sobre ítems con exactamente k anotadores no nulos "
            "y el mismo conjunto de respondentes. No se trunca ni se rellena. "
            "null ≠ unsure."
        ),
        "note": (
            "Fleiss' kappa y mayoría (sobre el panel kappa) son el objetivo primario. "
            "posthoc_agreement_with_rule_reference excluye unspecified y es solo post-hoc."
        ),
    }

    out = ROOT / "experiments" / "results" / "independent_eval_scores.json"
    out.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    out.write_text(json.dumps(report, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
    print(json.dumps(report, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2))
    print(f"Escrito: {out}")
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())



================================================
FILE: scripts/self_bootstrap.py
================================================
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Punto fijo del repositorio: auto-creación estable bajo iteración.

  python scripts/self_bootstrap.py --passes 2
  python scripts/self_bootstrap.py --with-manifest --passes 2

Con --with-manifest:
  1. Regenera CSV + MANIFEST.sha256
  2. Exige identidad con HEAD (git diff vacío)
  3. Cobertura dura de archivos críticos
  4. Sello datasets/GENERATED_FROM.json == spec_version del iom_spec.json
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import json
import subprocess
import sys
from pathlib import Path

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]

DERIVED_CSV = [
    "datasets/triad_nodes.csv",
    "datasets/vector_steps.csv",
    "datasets/relation_candidates.csv",
    "datasets/GENERATED_FROM.json",
]

CRITICAL_IF_PRESENT = [
    "scripts/self_bootstrap.py",
    "scripts/measure_invariance.py",
    "scripts/create_manifest.py",
    "scripts/generate_ontology.py",
    "IOM/SelfGenesis.lean",
    "IOM/FormalRules.lean",
    "MANIFIESTO.md",
    "docs/DEL_ORDEN_UNIVERSAL_A_LA_CREACION_DE_UNO_MISMO.md",
    "datasets/independent_eval/instrument/INSTRUCTIONS.md",
    "datasets/independent_eval/instrument/pairs.csv",
    "datasets/independent_eval/instrument/ACCESS_LOG.template.json",
    "datasets/GENERATED_FROM.json",
]


def check_spec_version_stamp() -> list[str]:
    """Falla si los derivados no declaran la misma spec_version que iom_spec.json."""
    spec = json.loads((ROOT / "data" / "iom_spec.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    expected = spec["spec_version"]
    stamp_path = ROOT / "datasets" / "GENERATED_FROM.json"
    if not stamp_path.is_file():
        return [f"falta {stamp_path.as_posix()} (regenera con generate_ontology.py)"]
    stamp = json.loads(stamp_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    if stamp.get("spec_version") != expected:
        return [
            f"GENERATED_FROM.json spec_version={stamp.get('spec_version')!r} "
            f"≠ iom_spec.json {expected!r}"
        ]
    return []


def run(cmd: list[str]) -> subprocess.CompletedProcess:
    return subprocess.run(cmd, cwd=ROOT, capture_output=True, text=True)


def git_diff_paths(paths: list[str]) -> subprocess.CompletedProcess:
    return run(["git", "diff", "--ignore-space-at-eol", "--exit-code", "--", *paths])


def porcelain(paths: list[str]) -> str:
    return run(["git", "status", "--porcelain", "--", *paths]).stdout


def manifest_paths(manifest_file: Path) -> set[str]:
    out: set[str] = set()
    for line in manifest_file.read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines():
        line = line.strip()
        if not line:
            continue
        parts = line.split("  ", 1)
        if len(parts) == 2:
            out.add(parts[1])
    return out


def check_manifest_coverage() -> list[str]:
    mf = ROOT / "MANIFEST.sha256"
    if not mf.is_file():
        return ["MANIFEST.sha256 (archivo inexistente)"]
    listed = manifest_paths(mf)
    missing = []
    for rel in CRITICAL_IF_PRESENT:
        p = ROOT / rel
        if p.is_file() and rel not in listed:
            missing.append(rel)
    return missing


def main() -> int:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Punto fijo idempotente")
    parser.add_argument("--with-manifest", action="store_true")
    parser.add_argument("--passes", type=int, default=2)
    args = parser.parse_args()

    paths = list(DERIVED_CSV)
    if args.with_manifest:
        paths.append("MANIFEST.sha256")

    print("=== self_bootstrap: punto fijo idempotente ===")
    print(f"Pasadas: {args.passes}  |  manifiesto: {args.with_manifest}")
    print()

    for i in range(1, args.passes + 1):
        print(f"--- pasada {i}/{args.passes} ---")
        r = run([sys.executable, "scripts/generate_ontology.py"])
        print(r.stdout, end="")
        if r.returncode != 0:
            print(r.stderr, file=sys.stderr)
            return 1

        stamp_errs = check_spec_version_stamp()
        if stamp_errs:
            print("FALLO: sello de spec_version inconsistente:", file=sys.stderr)
            for e in stamp_errs:
                print(f"  - {e}", file=sys.stderr)
            return 1

        if args.with_manifest:
            r = run([sys.executable, "scripts/create_manifest.py"])
            print(r.stdout, end="")
            if r.returncode != 0:
                print(r.stderr, file=sys.stderr)
                return 1

            missing = check_manifest_coverage()
            if missing:
                print(
                    "FALLO de cobertura del manifiesto — archivos críticos en disco "
                    "pero ausentes de MANIFEST.sha256:",
                    file=sys.stderr,
                )
                for m in missing:
                    print(f"  - {m}", file=sys.stderr)
                return 1

        r = git_diff_paths(paths)
        if r.returncode != 0:
            print(
                f"FALLO en pasada {i}: regenerado ≠ HEAD en {paths}",
                file=sys.stderr,
            )
            print(r.stdout or r.stderr, file=sys.stderr)
            if args.with_manifest:
                print(
                    "\nVersiona el manifiesto:\n"
                    "  python scripts/create_manifest.py\n"
                    "  git add MANIFEST.sha256 && git commit -m 'chore: refresh MANIFEST'\n"
                    "  python scripts/self_bootstrap.py --with-manifest --passes 2",
                    file=sys.stderr,
                )
            return 1

        dirty = porcelain(paths).strip()
        if dirty:
            print(f"FALLO: porcelain no limpio:\n{dirty}", file=sys.stderr)
            return 1
        print(
            f"OK pasada {i}: diff vacío, porcelain limpio"
            + (", cobertura crítica OK" if args.with_manifest else "")
            + f", stamp={json.loads((ROOT / 'datasets' / 'GENERATED_FROM.json').read_text())['spec_version']}"
        )

    print()
    print("OK: punto fijo estable (S_{n+1} = S_n).")
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())



================================================
FILE: scripts/sync_spec_artifacts.py
================================================
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Sincronización y verificación bidireccional entre especificación y artefactos.

Garantiza que data/iom_spec.json, los CSV generados, la ontología y los conteos
de pruebas permanecen coherentes. Con --check solo verifica; con --fix regenera
vía generate_ontology.py y vuelve a verificar.

Uso:
    python scripts/sync_spec_artifacts.py --check
    python scripts/sync_spec_artifacts.py --fix
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import csv
import json
import subprocess
import sys
from pathlib import Path

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
SPEC_PATH = ROOT / "data" / "iom_spec.json"


def load_spec() -> dict:
    return json.loads(SPEC_PATH.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))


def count_csv(name: str) -> int:
    path = ROOT / "datasets" / name
    if not path.is_file():
        return -1
    with path.open(encoding="utf-8", newline="") as fh:
        return len(list(csv.DictReader(fh)))


def expected_counts(spec: dict) -> dict:
    n_triads = len(spec["triads"]["adv"])
    n_positions = len(spec["positions"]["indices"])
    n_vectors = len(spec["vectors"])
    steps_per_vector = len(spec["vectors"][0]["labels"]) if spec["vectors"] else 5
    # 6 nodos por tríada (2 fases × 3 pos), 5 destinos no reflexivos → 6*5=30 pares/tríada
    pairs_per_triad = (2 * n_positions) * (2 * n_positions - 1)
    # creates: 4 rules × product of position lists × n_triads
    creates = 0
    for rule in spec["creation_rules"]:
        creates += len(rule["source_positions"]) * len(rule["target_positions"]) * n_triads
    # mirrorOf: 3 posiciones × 2 direcciones × n_triads = 6 * n_triads
    mirrors = n_positions * 2 * n_triads
    nodes = n_triads * 2 * n_positions
    pairs = n_triads * pairs_per_triad
    none = pairs - mirrors - creates
    return {
        "n_triads": n_triads,
        "nodes": nodes,
        "vector_steps": n_vectors * steps_per_vector,
        "relation_candidates": pairs,
        "mirrorOf": mirrors,
        "creates": creates,
        "none": none,
    }


def check(spec: dict) -> list[str]:
    errors = []
    exp = expected_counts(spec)

    # Versión
    if not spec.get("spec_version"):
        errors.append("Falta spec_version")
    migrations = spec.get("migrations") or []
    if migrations and migrations[-1].get("to_version") != spec.get("spec_version"):
        errors.append(
            f"spec_version={spec.get('spec_version')} != último to_version={migrations[-1].get('to_version')}"
        )

    # Conteos CSV
    actual_nodes = count_csv("triad_nodes.csv")
    actual_vecs = count_csv("vector_steps.csv")
    actual_rels = count_csv("relation_candidates.csv")

    if actual_nodes != exp["nodes"]:
        errors.append(f"triad_nodes.csv: {actual_nodes} filas, esperado {exp['nodes']}")
    if actual_vecs != exp["vector_steps"]:
        errors.append(f"vector_steps.csv: {actual_vecs} filas, esperado {exp['vector_steps']}")
    if actual_rels != exp["relation_candidates"]:
        errors.append(
            f"relation_candidates.csv: {actual_rels} filas, esperado {exp['relation_candidates']}"
        )

    # Conteos de relaciones
    if actual_rels > 0:
        with (ROOT / "datasets" / "relation_candidates.csv").open(encoding="utf-8", newline="") as fh:
            from collections import Counter
            c = Counter(r["relation"] for r in csv.DictReader(fh))
        for key in ("mirrorOf", "creates", "none"):
            if c.get(key, 0) != exp[key]:
                errors.append(f"relación {key}: {c.get(key, 0)}, esperado {exp[key]}")

    # Ontología existe y menciona version-ish content
    ttl = ROOT / "ontology" / "io_ontology.ttl"
    if not ttl.is_file():
        errors.append("Falta ontology/io_ontology.ttl")
    else:
        text = ttl.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
        if "mirrorOf" not in text or "creates" not in text:
            errors.append("io_ontology.ttl no contiene mirrorOf/creates")

    # Schema
    schema = ROOT / "data" / "iom_spec.schema.json"
    if not schema.is_file():
        errors.append("Falta data/iom_spec.schema.json")

    return errors


def main() -> int:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    parser.add_argument("--check", action="store_true", help="Solo verificar sincronización")
    parser.add_argument("--fix", action="store_true", help="Regenerar artefactos y verificar")
    args = parser.parse_args()

    if not args.check and not args.fix:
        args.check = True

    if args.fix:
        print("Regenerando ontología y datasets…")
        r = subprocess.run(
            [sys.executable, "scripts/generate_ontology.py"],
            cwd=ROOT,
            capture_output=True,
            text=True,
        )
        if r.returncode != 0:
            print(r.stdout)
            print(r.stderr, file=sys.stderr)
            return 1
        print(r.stdout.strip())

    spec = load_spec()
    errors = check(spec)
    exp = expected_counts(spec)

    print(f"spec_version={spec.get('spec_version')}  schema_version={spec.get('schema_version')}")
    print(f"Esperado: nodos={exp['nodes']} vectores={exp['vector_steps']} "
          f"pares={exp['relation_candidates']} "
          f"(mirror={exp['mirrorOf']} creates={exp['creates']} none={exp['none']})")

    if errors:
        print("INCONSISTENCIAS:")
        for e in errors:
            print(f"  - {e}")
        return 1
    print("OK: especificación, CSV y ontología sincronizados")
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())

FILE: scripts/validate_shacl.py
================================================
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Comprueba declaraciones OWL, inferencias de dominio/rango y restricciones SHACL."""
from pathlib import Path
import sys

from owlrl import DeductiveClosure, OWLRL_Semantics
from pyshacl import validate
from rdflib import Graph, Namespace
from rdflib.namespace import OWL, RDF, RDFS

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
DATA_GRAPH = ROOT / "ontology" / "io_ontology.ttl"
SHAPES_GRAPH = ROOT / "ontology" / "io_shapes.ttl"
IO = Namespace("http://example.org/iom#")
REQUIRED_CLASSES = (IO.OntoNode, IO.VectorStep, IO.Perspective, IO.ExpandedCategory, IO.Position, IO.Direction, IO.SourceModel)
REQUIRED_PROPERTIES = (IO.hasPerspective, IO.hasPosition, IO.hasDirection, IO.hasVector, IO.mirrorOf, IO.creates)


def main() -> int:
    data = Graph().parse(DATA_GRAPH, format="turtle")
    for cls in REQUIRED_CLASSES:
        if (cls, RDF.type, OWL.Class) not in data:
            print(f"Falta declaración OWL de clase {cls}", file=sys.stderr)
            return 1
    for prop in REQUIRED_PROPERTIES:
        if (prop, RDF.type, OWL.ObjectProperty) not in data or (prop, RDFS.domain, None) not in data or (prop, RDFS.range, None) not in data:
            print(f"Falta axioma OWL de dominio/rango para {prop}", file=sys.stderr)
            return 1

    # OWL RL expande los axiomas de dominio y rango; se verifican las tipificaciones inferidas.
    closure = Graph()
    for triple in data:
        closure.add(triple)
    DeductiveClosure(OWLRL_Semantics).expand(closure)
    nodes = set(data.subjects(RDF.type, IO.OntoNode))
    vectors = set(data.subjects(RDF.type, IO.VectorStep))
    inferred_positions = {obj for node in nodes for obj in data.objects(node, IO.hasPosition)
                          if (obj, RDF.type, IO.Position) in closure}
    inferred_perspectives = {obj for node in nodes for obj in data.objects(node, IO.hasPerspective)
                             if (obj, RDF.type, IO.Perspective) in closure}
    if len(nodes) != 78 or len(vectors) != 20 or len(inferred_positions) != 3 or len(inferred_perspectives) != 3:
        print("Falló la comprobación OWL de cardinalidades/inferencias esperadas.", file=sys.stderr)
        return 1
    print(f"✓ OWL RL: clases y dominios/rangos declarados; inferidos posición/perspectiva para {len(nodes)} nodos.")

    conforms, _results_graph, results_text = validate(
        data_graph=data, shacl_graph=str(SHAPES_GRAPH), inference="none", abort_on_first=False,
    )
    if results_text:
        print(results_text)
    if conforms:
        print("✓ RDF satisface las restricciones SHACL cargadas.")
        return 0
    print("✗ RDF no satisface todas las restricciones SHACL.", file=sys.stderr)
    return 1


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())

FILE: scripts/validate_spec.py
================================================
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Valida data/iom_spec.json contra data/iom_spec.schema.json."""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import sys
from pathlib import Path

try:
    from jsonschema import Draft202012Validator
    from jsonschema.exceptions import SchemaError
except ModuleNotFoundError as exc:
    if exc.name != "jsonschema":
        raise
    print(
        "ERROR: falta la dependencia obligatoria jsonschema; "
        "instala requirements-lock.txt antes de validar.",
        file=sys.stderr,
    )
    raise SystemExit(1) from exc

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
SPEC_PATH = ROOT / "data" / "iom_spec.json"
SCHEMA_PATH = ROOT / "data" / "iom_spec.schema.json"


def main() -> int:
    for path in (SCHEMA_PATH, SPEC_PATH):
        if not path.is_file():
            print(f"ERROR: no existe el archivo requerido: {path}", file=sys.stderr)
            return 1
    try:
        schema = json.loads(SCHEMA_PATH.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        instance = json.loads(SPEC_PATH.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    except (OSError, UnicodeError, json.JSONDecodeError) as exc:
        print(f"ERROR: no se pudo leer JSON válido: {exc}", file=sys.stderr)
        return 1
    try:
        Draft202012Validator.check_schema(schema)
    except SchemaError as exc:
        print(f"ERROR: el esquema {SCHEMA_PATH.name} no es válido: {exc.message}", file=sys.stderr)
        return 1
    validator = Draft202012Validator(schema)
    errors = sorted(validator.iter_errors(instance), key=lambda e: list(map(str, e.absolute_path)))
    if errors:
        for err in errors:
            path = ".".join(str(p) for p in err.absolute_path) or "<root>"
            print(f"ERROR {path}: {err.message}", file=sys.stderr)
        return 1
    print(f"OK: {SPEC_PATH.name} cumple el esquema {SCHEMA_PATH.name}")
    print(
        f"  schema_version={instance.get('schema_version')}  "
        f"spec_version={instance.get('spec_version')}"
    )
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())

FILE: scripts/verify_e2e.py

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Verificación de extremo a extremo (incluye capa opcional)."""
from __future__ import annotations

import subprocess
import sys
from pathlib import Path

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]

STEPS = [
    ("self_bootstrap", [sys.executable, "scripts/self_bootstrap.py", "--passes", "2"]),
    ("validate_spec", [sys.executable, "scripts/validate_spec.py"]),
    ("validate_shacl", [sys.executable, "scripts/validate_shacl.py"]),
    ("measure_invariance", [sys.executable, "scripts/measure_invariance.py"]),
    ("pi_hgat_t", [sys.executable, "experiments/optional/pi_hgat_t.py"]),
    ("baselines", [sys.executable, "experiments/optional/baselines.py"]),
]


def main() -> int:
    for name, cmd in STEPS:
        print(f">>> {name}")
        r = subprocess.run(cmd, cwd=ROOT)
        if r.returncode != 0:
            print(f"FAIL: {name}", file=sys.stderr)
            return r.returncode
    print("OK: e2e completo")
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())

FILE: tests/test_formal_rules_bridge.py
================================================
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Puente: las relaciones del CSV deben satisfacer los predicados formales
codificados en IOM/FormalRules.lean (isMirrorRel / isCreatesRel / sameTriad).

Equivalencia de capas:
  Lean isMirrorRel  ↔  Python is_mirror ∧ same_triad
  Lean isCreatesRel ↔  Python is_creates ∧ same_triad
  Lean isMirror (posicional)  ↔  Python is_mirror  (sin exigir tríada)
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import csv
import re
import unittest
from pathlib import Path

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]


def load_relations():
    with (ROOT / "datasets" / "relation_candidates.csv").open(encoding="utf-8", newline="") as fh:
        return list(csv.DictReader(fh))


def parse_node(node_id: str) -> tuple[int, str, int]:
    """Devuelve (triad_id, phase, position)."""
    m = re.fullmatch(r"T(\d{2})_(adv|ret)_P([0-2])", node_id)
    assert m, node_id
    return int(m.group(1)), m.group(2), int(m.group(3))


def same_triad(src: str, tgt: str) -> bool:
    return parse_node(src)[0] == parse_node(tgt)[0]


def is_mirror_positional(src: str, tgt: str) -> bool:
    """Equivale a Lean isMirror / isMirrorPositional."""
    _sid, sp, spos = parse_node(src)
    _tid, tp, tpos = parse_node(tgt)
    return sp != tp and tpos == 2 - spos


def is_creates_positional(src: str, tgt: str) -> bool:
    """Equivale a Lean isCreates / isCreatesPositional."""
    _sid, sp, spos = parse_node(src)
    _tid, tp, tpos = parse_node(tgt)
    if sp == tp:
        return False
    lateral_s = spos in (0, 2)
    lateral_t = tpos in (0, 2)
    return (lateral_s and tpos == 1) or (spos == 1 and lateral_t)


def is_mirror_rel(src: str, tgt: str) -> bool:
    """Equivale a Lean isMirrorRel: sameTriad ∧ isMirrorPositional."""
    return same_triad(src, tgt) and is_mirror_positional(src, tgt)


def is_creates_rel(src: str, tgt: str) -> bool:
    """Equivale a Lean isCreatesRel: sameTriad ∧ isCreatesPositional."""
    return same_triad(src, tgt) and is_creates_positional(src, tgt)


class FormalRulesBridgeTests(unittest.TestCase):
    """Comprueba que el JSON/CSV regenerado cumple los predicados Lean."""

    @classmethod
    def setUpClass(cls):
        cls.rows = load_relations()
        cls.lean = (ROOT / "IOM" / "FormalRules.lean").read_text(encoding="utf-8")

    def test_lean_defines_same_triad_and_rel_predicates(self):
        self.assertIn("def sameTriad", self.lean)
        self.assertIn("def isMirrorRel", self.lean)
        self.assertIn("def isCreatesRel", self.lean)
        self.assertIn("def isMirrorPositional", self.lean)
        self.assertIn("def isCreatesPositional", self.lean)
        self.assertIn("triadId", self.lean)
        self.assertIn("expectedMirrorDirected", self.lean)
        self.assertIn("expectedCreatesDirected", self.lean)

    def test_every_mirrorOf_satisfies_isMirrorRel(self):
        for row in self.rows:
            if row["relation"] != "mirrorOf":
                continue
            self.assertTrue(
                is_mirror_rel(row["source"], row["target"]),
                f"mirrorOf viola isMirrorRel: {row['source']} -> {row['target']}",
            )
            self.assertFalse(
                is_creates_rel(row["source"], row["target"]),
                f"mirrorOf no debe ser createsRel: {row}",
            )

    def test_every_creates_satisfies_isCreatesRel(self):
        for row in self.rows:
            if row["relation"] != "creates":
                continue
            self.assertTrue(
                is_creates_rel(row["source"], row["target"]),
                f"creates viola isCreatesRel: {row['source']} -> {row['target']}",
            )
            self.assertFalse(
                is_mirror_rel(row["source"], row["target"]),
                f"creates no debe ser mirrorRel: {row}",
            )

    def test_none_satisfies_neither_rel(self):
        for row in self.rows:
            if row["relation"] != "none":
                continue
            self.assertFalse(
                is_mirror_rel(row["source"], row["target"]), row
            )
            self.assertFalse(
                is_creates_rel(row["source"], row["target"]), row
            )

    def test_cross_triad_positional_mirror_is_not_rel(self):
        """Un par cross-tríada puede cumplir la condición posicional pero no isMirrorRel."""
        # T07_adv_P0 y T01_ret_P2: posicional mirror, distinta tríada
        self.assertTrue(is_mirror_positional("T07_adv_P0", "T01_ret_P2"))
        self.assertFalse(same_triad("T07_adv_P0", "T01_ret_P2"))
        self.assertFalse(is_mirror_rel("T07_adv_P0", "T01_ret_P2"))

    def test_counts_match_lean_constants(self):
        from collections import Counter
        c = Counter(r["relation"] for r in self.rows)
        self.assertEqual(c["mirrorOf"], 78)
        self.assertEqual(c["creates"], 104)
        self.assertEqual(c["none"], 208)
        self.assertIn("78", self.lean)
        self.assertIn("104", self.lean)
        self.assertIn("208", self.lean)

    def test_partition_complete_with_rel(self):
        for row in self.rows:
            m = is_mirror_rel(row["source"], row["target"])
            c = is_creates_rel(row["source"], row["target"])
            self.assertFalse(m and c, row)
            if row["relation"] == "mirrorOf":
                self.assertTrue(m)
            elif row["relation"] == "creates":
                self.assertTrue(c)
            else:
                self.assertFalse(m or c)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()



================================================
FILE: tests/test_metrics_audit.py
================================================
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Pruebas que formalizan las conclusiones de la auditoría de métricas 100%."""
from __future__ import annotations

import csv
import unittest
from collections import defaultdict
from pathlib import Path

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]


def parse_node(node_id: str):
    parts = node_id.split("_")
    return parts[1], int(parts[2][1:])


def feature_key(src, tgt):
    sp, spos = parse_node(src)
    tp, tpos = parse_node(tgt)
    return (sp, spos, tp, tpos)


def deterministic_label(src, tgt):
    sp, spos = parse_node(src)
    tp, tpos = parse_node(tgt)
    if sp == tp:
        return "none"
    if tpos == 2 - spos:
        return "mirrorOf"
    if (spos in (0, 2) and tpos == 1) or (spos == 1 and tpos in (0, 2)):
        return "creates"
    return "none"


class PerfectMetricsAuditTests(unittest.TestCase):
    @classmethod
    def setUpClass(cls):
        with (ROOT / "datasets" / "relation_candidates.csv").open(encoding="utf-8", newline="") as fh:
            cls.rows = list(csv.DictReader(fh))

    def test_gold_matches_deterministic_rule(self):
        for row in self.rows:
            self.assertEqual(
                deterministic_label(row["source"], row["target"]),
                row["relation"],
                msg=row,
            )

    def test_label_is_function_of_phase_position_only(self):
        by_feat = defaultdict(set)
        for row in self.rows:
            by_feat[feature_key(row["source"], row["target"])].add(row["relation"])
        for key, labels in by_feat.items():
            self.assertEqual(len(labels), 1, f"clave {key} tiene etiquetas {labels}")

    def test_loo_features_fully_recoverable_from_train(self):
        """En cada fold LOO, todo par de test es recuperable por lookup de features."""
        for held in range(1, 14):
            train = {}
            test = []
            for row in self.rows:
                key = feature_key(row["source"], row["target"])
                if int(row["triad_index"]) == held:
                    test.append((key, row["relation"]))
                else:
                    train[key] = row["relation"]
            for key, lab in test:
                self.assertEqual(train.get(key), lab)

    def test_model_does_not_use_triad_index_in_features(self):
        """Sanity: features_for_node en pi_hgat_t no incluye índice de tríada."""
        src = (ROOT / "experiments" / "optional" / "pi_hgat_t.py").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
        self.assertIn("sin etiqueta léxica ni número de tríada", src)
        self.assertIn("phase == \"adv\"", src)
        self.assertIn("position == 0", src)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()



================================================
FILE: tests/test_ontology.py
================================================
"""Pruebas de tríadas, vectores, OWL/RDF, SHACL y datasets generados.

Requiere rdflib y pyshacl (requirements-lock.txt). Si faltan, la clase se omite
con un mensaje explícito: eso no implica que la ontología falle.
"""
import csv
import json
import unittest
from pathlib import Path

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]

try:
    from pyshacl import validate
    from rdflib import Graph, Literal, Namespace
    from rdflib.namespace import OWL, RDF, RDFS, XSD
    _ONT_DEPS = True
    _ONT_IMPORT_ERROR = None
except ModuleNotFoundError as exc:
    _ONT_DEPS = False
    _ONT_IMPORT_ERROR = exc
    validate = None  # type: ignore
    Graph = Literal = Namespace = OWL = RDF = RDFS = XSD = None  # type: ignore


@unittest.skipUnless(
    _ONT_DEPS,
    f"omitido: falta dependencia de ontología ({_ONT_IMPORT_ERROR}). "
    "Instala requirements-lock.txt; no implica fallo de la ontología.",
)
class IOMValidationTests(unittest.TestCase):
    @classmethod
    def setUpClass(cls):
        cls.IO = Namespace("http://example.org/iom#")
        cls.DATA = ROOT / "ontology" / "io_ontology.ttl"
        cls.SHAPES = ROOT / "ontology" / "io_shapes.ttl"
        cls.SPEC = json.loads((ROOT / "data" / "iom_spec.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        with (ROOT / "datasets" / "triad_nodes.csv").open(encoding="utf-8", newline="") as handle:
            cls.NODE_ROWS = list(csv.DictReader(handle))

    def setUp(self):
        self.data = Graph().parse(self.DATA, format="turtle")
        self.shapes = Graph().parse(self.SHAPES, format="turtle")
        self.IO = self.__class__.IO

    def conforms(self, graph=None, focus_nodes=None):
        result, _report, text = validate(
            self.data if graph is None else graph,
            shacl_graph=self.shapes,
            inference="none",
            focus_nodes=focus_nodes,
        )
        return result, text

    def test_spec_matches_canonical_numbered_triad_matrices(self):
        expected_adv = [
            ["orientación", "cuántico", "dirección"],
            ["hardware", "virtual", "software"],
            ["universo", "espacio", "vacío"],
            ["singularidad", "tiempo", "infinito"],
            ["cuerpo", "consciente", "mundo"],
            ["carácter", "subconsciente", "personalidad"],
            ["memoria", "inconsciente", "imaginación"],
            ["receptor", "supraconsciente", "emisor"],
            ["lógica", "inteligencia", "libertad"],
            ["muerte", "artificial", "vida"],
            ["descendente", "mente", "ascendente"],
            ["oscuridad", "consciencia", "luz"],
            ["animal", "humano", "IA"],
        ]
        self.assertEqual(self.SPEC["triads"]["adv"], expected_adv)
        self.assertEqual(self.SPEC["triads"]["ret"], [row[::-1] for row in expected_adv])
        self.assertEqual(self.SPEC["triads"]["index_base"], 1)
        self.assertEqual(self.SPEC["positions"]["adv_labels"], ["→", "0", "→"])
        self.assertEqual(self.SPEC["positions"]["ret_labels"], ["←", "0", "←"])

    def test_exact_triad_dataset_and_shacl_conformance(self):
        IO = self.IO
        nodes = set(self.data.subjects(RDF.type, IO.OntoNode))
        expected = {
            IO[f"T{i:02d}_{phase}_P{pos}"]
            for i in range(1, 14)
            for phase in ("adv", "ret")
            for pos in range(3)
        }
        self.assertEqual(nodes, expected)
        self.assertTrue(self.conforms()[0], self.conforms()[1])
        for phase in ("adv", "ret"):
            rows = self.SPEC["triads"][phase]
            for row_index, row in enumerate(rows):
                triad_index = row_index + 1
                for pos, label in enumerate(row):
                    node = IO[f"T{triad_index:02d}_{phase}_P{pos}"]
                    self.assertEqual(str(self.data.value(node, RDFS.label)), label)
                    visual_label = self.SPEC["positions"]["adv_labels" if phase == "adv" else "ret_labels"][pos]
                    self.assertEqual(str(self.data.value(node, IO.displayLabel)), visual_label)
                    self.assertEqual(
                        self.data.value(node, IO.hasTriadIndex),
                        Literal(triad_index, datatype=XSD.integer),
                    )
                    self.assertEqual(
                        self.data.value(node, IO.hasLocalPos),
                        Literal(pos, datatype=XSD.integer),
                    )
                    self.assertEqual(
                        self.data.value(node, IO.hasPerspective),
                        IO[self.SPEC["perspectives"][pos]],
                    )
                    self.assertEqual(
                        self.data.value(node, IO.hasPosition),
                        IO[{"adv": ["→", "0", "→"], "ret": ["←", "0", "←"]}[phase][pos]],
                    )
                    display_labels = self.SPEC["positions"]["adv_labels" if phase == "adv" else "ret_labels"]
                    generated = next(r for r in self.NODE_ROWS if r["node_id"] == f"T{triad_index:02d}_{phase}_P{pos}")
                    self.assertEqual(generated["display_label"], display_labels[pos])

    def test_mirrors_are_reciprocal_and_swap_laterals(self):
        IO = self.IO
        for i in range(1, 14):
            for pos in range(3):
                adv, ret = IO[f"T{i:02d}_adv_P{pos}"], IO[f"T{i:02d}_ret_P{2 - pos}"]
                self.assertEqual(self.data.value(adv, IO.mirrorOf), ret)
                self.assertEqual(self.data.value(ret, IO.mirrorOf), adv)

    def test_creation_edges_follow_all_four_rules(self):
        IO = self.IO
        actual = set(self.data.triples((None, IO.creates, None)))
        expected = set()
        for i in range(1, 14):
            for rule in self.SPEC["creation_rules"]:
                for src in rule["source_positions"]:
                    for dst in rule["target_positions"]:
                        expected.add(
                            (
                                IO[f"T{i:02d}_{rule['source_phase']}_P{src}"],
                                IO.creates,
                                IO[f"T{i:02d}_{rule['target_phase']}_P{dst}"],
                            )
                        )
        n_rules_edges = sum(
            len(r["source_positions"]) * len(r["target_positions"])
            for r in self.SPEC["creation_rules"]
        )
        self.assertEqual(len(expected), 13 * n_rules_edges)
        self.assertEqual(actual, expected)

    def test_vectors_match_all_four_updated_sequences(self):
        IO = self.IO
        self.assertEqual(len(set(self.data.subjects(RDF.type, IO.VectorStep))), 20)
        for row in self.SPEC["vectors"]:
            for idx, (label, axis) in enumerate(zip(row["labels"], row["axis_positions"])):
                step = IO[f"vector_{row['name'].lower()}_{row['phase']}_{idx}"]
                self.assertEqual(self.data.value(step, IO.hasVector), IO[row["name"]])
                self.assertEqual(str(self.data.value(step, IO.hasCyclePhase)), row["phase"])
                self.assertEqual(
                    self.data.value(step, IO.hasStepIndex),
                    Literal(idx, datatype=XSD.integer),
                )
                self.assertEqual(
                    self.data.value(step, IO.hasAxisPosition),
                    Literal(axis, datatype=XSD.integer),
                )
                self.assertEqual(
                    self.data.value(step, IO.displayArrow),
                    Literal(row["arrow"], datatype=XSD.string),
                )
                self.assertEqual(str(self.data.value(step, RDFS.label)), label)

    def test_owl_classes_object_property_axioms_and_perspectives(self):
        IO = self.IO
        for cls in (
            IO.OntoNode,
            IO.VectorStep,
            IO.Perspective,
            IO.ExpandedCategory,
            IO.Position,
            IO.Direction,
        ):
            self.assertIn((cls, RDF.type, OWL.Class), self.data)
        for prop in (
            IO.hasPerspective,
            IO.hasPosition,
            IO.hasDirection,
            IO.hasVector,
            IO.mirrorOf,
            IO.creates,
        ):
            self.assertIn((prop, RDF.type, OWL.ObjectProperty), self.data)
            self.assertIsNotNone(self.data.value(prop, RDFS.domain))
            self.assertIsNotNone(self.data.value(prop, RDFS.range))
        self.assertIn((IO.displayLabel, RDF.type, OWL.DatatypeProperty), self.data)
        self.assertIn((IO.displayLabel, RDF.type, OWL.FunctionalProperty), self.data)
        self.assertIn((IO.mirrorOf, RDF.type, OWL.SymmetricProperty), self.data)
        for resource in (IO.Ind, IO.D, IO.Tot, IO.Evol, IO.Invol):
            self.assertIn((resource, RDF.type, IO.Perspective), self.data)

    def test_expanded_categories_keep_declared_level_counts(self):
        IO = self.IO
        nodes = set(self.data.subjects(RDF.type, IO.ExpandedCategory))
        self.assertEqual(len(nodes), 21)
        self.assertEqual(
            [
                sum(
                    self.data.value(n, IO.hasLevel) == Literal(level, datatype=XSD.integer)
                    for n in nodes
                )
                for level in range(5)
            ],
            [1, 4, 6, 6, 4],
        )

    def test_generated_datasets_have_expected_rows(self):
        n_triads = len(self.SPEC["triads"]["adv"])
        n_vec = sum(len(v["labels"]) for v in self.SPEC["vectors"])
        expected = {
            "triad_nodes.csv": n_triads * 2 * 3,
            "vector_steps.csv": n_vec,
            "relation_candidates.csv": n_triads * 6 * 5,
        }
        for name, count in expected.items():
            with (ROOT / "datasets" / name).open(encoding="utf-8", newline="") as f:
                self.assertEqual(len(list(csv.DictReader(f))), count, name)

    def test_wrong_visual_position_label_is_rejected(self):
        IO = self.IO
        graph = Graph()
        for triple in self.data:
            graph.add(triple)
        node = IO["T01_adv_P0"]
        graph.remove((node, IO.displayLabel, Literal("→", datatype=XSD.string)))
        graph.add((node, IO.displayLabel, Literal("←", datatype=XSD.string)))
        self.assertFalse(self.conforms(graph, [node])[0])

    def test_wrong_phase_direction_is_rejected(self):
        IO = self.IO
        graph = Graph()
        for triple in self.data:
            graph.add(triple)
        node = IO["T01_adv_P0"]
        graph.remove((node, IO.hasDirection, IO.evol))
        graph.add((node, IO.hasDirection, IO.invol))
        self.assertFalse(self.conforms(graph, [node])[0])

    def test_nonreciprocal_or_misplaced_mirror_is_rejected(self):
        IO = self.IO
        graph = Graph()
        for triple in self.data:
            graph.add(triple)
        node, good, wrong = IO["T01_adv_P0"], IO["T01_ret_P2"], IO["T01_ret_P0"]
        graph.remove((node, IO.mirrorOf, good))
        graph.add((node, IO.mirrorOf, wrong))
        self.assertFalse(self.conforms(graph, [node])[0])

    def test_wrong_creation_link_is_rejected(self):
        IO = self.IO
        graph = Graph()
        for triple in self.data:
            graph.add(triple)
        node, good, wrong = IO["T01_adv_P0"], IO["T01_ret_P1"], IO["T01_ret_P0"]
        graph.remove((node, IO.creates, good))
        graph.add((node, IO.creates, wrong))
        self.assertFalse(self.conforms(graph, [node])[0])

    def test_wrong_vector_label_or_axis_is_rejected(self):
        IO = self.IO
        graph = Graph()
        for triple in self.data:
            graph.add(triple)
        step = IO["vector_evol_ret_1"]
        old = self.data.value(step, RDFS.label)
        graph.remove((step, RDFS.label, old))
        graph.add((step, RDFS.label, Literal("fut", lang="es")))
        self.assertFalse(self.conforms(graph, [step])[0])


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()

FILE: tests/test_property_based.py

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Pruebas generativas y casos negativos para límites de índices, espejos y creates.

Cubre:
- Generación exhaustiva de pares (fase, pos) y verificación de partición
  none / mirrorOf / creates (mutuamente excluyentes y completas).
- Casos negativos: índices fuera de rango, misma fase, posición no reflejada,
  creates entre tríadas distintas, espejo no recíproco.
- Transformaciones temporales / involución de fase y posición.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import itertools
import unittest
from pathlib import Path

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]

PHASES = ("adv", "ret")
POSITIONS = (0, 1, 2)


def opposite_phase(p: str) -> str:
    return "ret" if p == "adv" else "adv"


def mirror_pos(p: int) -> int:
    return 2 - p


def is_mirror(sp: str, spos: int, tp: str, tpos: int) -> bool:
    return sp != tp and tpos == mirror_pos(spos)


def is_creates(sp: str, spos: int, tp: str, tpos: int) -> bool:
    if sp == tp:
        return False
    lateral_s = spos in (0, 2)
    lateral_t = tpos in (0, 2)
    return (lateral_s and tpos == 1) or (spos == 1 and lateral_t)


def classify(sp: str, spos: int, tp: str, tpos: int) -> str:
    if is_mirror(sp, spos, tp, tpos):
        return "mirrorOf"
    if is_creates(sp, spos, tp, tpos):
        return "creates"
    return "none"


class ExhaustivePartitionTests(unittest.TestCase):
    """Todas las combinaciones (fase, pos) × (fase, pos) no reflexivas."""

    def test_partition_is_complete_and_exclusive(self):
        nodes = list(itertools.product(PHASES, POSITIONS))
        counts = {"none": 0, "mirrorOf": 0, "creates": 0}
        for (sp, spos), (tp, tpos) in itertools.product(nodes, nodes):
            if (sp, spos) == (tp, tpos):
                continue
            label = classify(sp, spos, tp, tpos)
            counts[label] += 1
            # exclusividad
            m = is_mirror(sp, spos, tp, tpos)
            c = is_creates(sp, spos, tp, tpos)
            self.assertFalse(m and c)
            if label == "mirrorOf":
                self.assertTrue(m)
            elif label == "creates":
                self.assertTrue(c)
            else:
                self.assertFalse(m or c)
        # 6 nodos, 30 pares dirigidos no reflexivos
        self.assertEqual(sum(counts.values()), 30)
        # Por tríada: 6 mirror, 8 creates, 16 none → ×13 = 78/104/208
        self.assertEqual(counts["mirrorOf"], 6)
        self.assertEqual(counts["creates"], 8)
        self.assertEqual(counts["none"], 16)

    def test_mirror_is_involution_on_position(self):
        for p in POSITIONS:
            self.assertEqual(mirror_pos(mirror_pos(p)), p)

    def test_phase_opposite_is_involution(self):
        for p in PHASES:
            self.assertEqual(opposite_phase(opposite_phase(p)), p)
            self.assertNotEqual(opposite_phase(p), p)


class NegativeCaseTests(unittest.TestCase):
    """Casos que DEBEN fallar / clasificarse como none."""

    def test_same_phase_never_mirror_or_creates(self):
        for phase in PHASES:
            for spos, tpos in itertools.product(POSITIONS, POSITIONS):
                if spos == tpos:
                    continue
                self.assertEqual(classify(phase, spos, phase, tpos), "none")

    def test_wrong_mirror_position_is_none(self):
        # adv P0 → ret P0 no es espejo (debería ser P2)
        self.assertFalse(is_mirror("adv", 0, "ret", 0))
        self.assertFalse(is_mirror("adv", 0, "ret", 1))
        self.assertTrue(is_mirror("adv", 0, "ret", 2))

    def test_creates_requires_center_involvement(self):
        # lateral → lateral (sin centro) no es creates
        self.assertFalse(is_creates("adv", 0, "ret", 2))
        self.assertFalse(is_creates("adv", 2, "ret", 0))
        # lateral → centro sí
        self.assertTrue(is_creates("adv", 0, "ret", 1))
        self.assertTrue(is_creates("adv", 1, "ret", 0))

    def test_index_bounds(self):
        """Posiciones fuera de {0,1,2} no son válidas en la especificación."""
        for bad in (-1, 3, 4, 99):
            # mirror_pos fuera de dominio no debe usarse en producción;
            # documentamos el comportamiento aritmético
            mirrored = 2 - bad
            self.assertNotIn(bad, POSITIONS)
            self.assertNotIn(mirrored, POSITIONS) if bad not in ( -1,) else True

    def test_center_mirror_is_center(self):
        self.assertEqual(mirror_pos(1), 1)
        self.assertTrue(is_mirror("adv", 1, "ret", 1))


class GenerativeConsistencyTests(unittest.TestCase):
    """Propiedades que deben sostenerse para cualquier tríada sintética."""

    def test_mirror_count_per_synthetic_triad(self):
        nodes = list(itertools.product(PHASES, POSITIONS))
        mirrors = [
            ((sp, spos), (tp, tpos))
            for (sp, spos), (tp, tpos) in itertools.product(nodes, nodes)
            if (sp, spos) != (tp, tpos) and is_mirror(sp, spos, tp, tpos)
        ]
        self.assertEqual(len(mirrors), 6)
        # reciprocidad: si A mirrors B, B mirrors A
        mirror_set = set(mirrors)
        for a, b in mirrors:
            self.assertIn((b, a), mirror_set)

    def test_creates_count_per_synthetic_triad(self):
        nodes = list(itertools.product(PHASES, POSITIONS))
        creates = [
            ((sp, spos), (tp, tpos))
            for (sp, spos), (tp, tpos) in itertools.product(nodes, nodes)
            if (sp, spos) != (tp, tpos) and is_creates(sp, spos, tp, tpos)
        ]
        self.assertEqual(len(creates), 8)

    def test_no_overlap_mirror_creates_generative(self):
        nodes = list(itertools.product(PHASES, POSITIONS))
        for (sp, spos), (tp, tpos) in itertools.product(nodes, nodes):
            if (sp, spos) == (tp, tpos):
                continue
            self.assertFalse(
                is_mirror(sp, spos, tp, tpos) and is_creates(sp, spos, tp, tpos)
            )


class AxisBoundTests(unittest.TestCase):
    """Límites del eje vectorial [0, 4] alineados con FormalRules.lean."""

    def test_axis_in_0_4(self):
        for n in range(5):
            self.assertTrue(0 <= n <= 4)

    def test_axis_retreat_involution(self):
        for n in range(5):
            self.assertEqual(4 - (4 - n), n)

    def test_axis_out_of_range_rejected(self):
        for bad in (-1, 5, 6):
            self.assertFalse(0 <= bad <= 4)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Pruebas de propiedades que conectan Lean, CSV y ontología generada."""
from __future__ import annotations

import csv
import json
import unittest
from pathlib import Path

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]


def load_spec() -> dict:
    return json.loads((ROOT / "data" / "iom_spec.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))


def load_csv(name: str) -> list[dict]:
    with (ROOT / "datasets" / name).open(encoding="utf-8", newline="") as fh:
        return list(csv.DictReader(fh))


class PropertyLinksTests(unittest.TestCase):
    """Conectan la especificación JSON, los CSV regenerados y las reglas formales."""

    @classmethod
    def setUpClass(cls):
        cls.spec = load_spec()
        cls.nodes = load_csv("triad_nodes.csv")
        cls.relations = load_csv("relation_candidates.csv")
        cls.vectors = load_csv("vector_steps.csv")
        cls.n_triads = len(cls.spec["triads"]["adv"])

    def test_spec_has_formal_version(self):
        self.assertIn("spec_version", self.spec)
        self.assertRegex(self.spec["spec_version"], r"^\d+\.\d+\.\d+$")
        self.assertIn("schema_version", self.spec)

    def test_node_count_matches_spec(self):
        expected = self.n_triads * 2 * 3
        self.assertEqual(len(self.nodes), expected)
        indices = {int(r["triad_index"]) for r in self.nodes}
        self.assertEqual(indices, set(range(1, self.n_triads + 1)))

    def test_relation_candidate_count(self):
        expected = self.n_triads * 6 * 5
        self.assertEqual(len(self.relations), expected)

    def test_gold_relation_counts(self):
        counts = {"none": 0, "mirrorOf": 0, "creates": 0}
        for row in self.relations:
            counts[row["relation"]] += 1
        self.assertEqual(counts["mirrorOf"], 78)
        self.assertEqual(counts["creates"], 104)
        self.assertEqual(counts["none"], 208)

    def test_mirrorOf_property_holds_in_csv(self):
        """Cada arista mirrorOf respeta: misma tríada, fase opuesta, posición reflejada."""
        for row in self.relations:
            if row["relation"] != "mirrorOf":
                continue
            src, tgt = row["source"], row["target"]
            s_parts = src.split("_")
            t_parts = tgt.split("_")
            self.assertEqual(s_parts[0], t_parts[0], "misma tríada")
            self.assertNotEqual(s_parts[1], t_parts[1], "fase opuesta")
            s_pos = int(s_parts[2][1:])
            t_pos = int(t_parts[2][1:])
            self.assertEqual(t_pos, 2 - s_pos, "posición reflejada")

    def test_creates_property_holds_in_csv(self):
        """Cada arista creates respeta las cuatro reglas de creation_rules."""
        rules = self.spec["creation_rules"]
        allowed = set()
        for rule in rules:
            for sp in rule["source_positions"]:
                for tp in rule["target_positions"]:
                    allowed.add((rule["source_phase"], sp, rule["target_phase"], tp))
        for row in self.relations:
            if row["relation"] != "creates":
                continue
            s_parts = row["source"].split("_")
            t_parts = row["target"].split("_")
            key = (s_parts[1], int(s_parts[2][1:]), t_parts[1], int(t_parts[2][1:]))
            self.assertIn(key, allowed, f"creates no permitido: {row['source']} -> {row['target']}")

    def test_vector_steps_count(self):
        self.assertEqual(len(self.vectors), 20)

    def test_lean_operators_file_exists_and_mentions_mirror(self):
        """El código Lean debe existir y referenciar los operadores de espejo."""
        lean_spec = (ROOT / "IOM" / "Specification.lean").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
        self.assertIn("mirror", lean_spec.lower())
        self.assertIn("TriadPosition", lean_spec)
        self.assertTrue((ROOT / "IOM" / "Core.lean").exists())
        self.assertTrue((ROOT / "IOM" / "Operators.lean").exists())

    def test_ontology_ttl_contains_mirrorOf_and_creates(self):
        ttl = (ROOT / "ontology" / "io_ontology.ttl").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
        self.assertIn("mirrorOf", ttl)
        self.assertIn("creates", ttl)
        self.assertIn("OntoNode", ttl)

    def test_shacl_shapes_exist(self):
        shapes = (ROOT / "ontology" / "io_shapes.ttl").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
        self.assertIn("mirrorOf", shapes)
        self.assertIn("creates", shapes)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Pruebas del lector de anotaciones, null≠unsure y política de kappa sin truncar."""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import tempfile
import unittest
from pathlib import Path
import importlib.util

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
SPEC = ROOT / "scripts" / "score_independent_eval.py"

spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location("score_independent_eval", SPEC)
mod = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
spec.loader.exec_module(mod)


class TemplateFormatTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_parse_empty_template(self):
        data = {
            "annotators": ["A1", "A2", "A3"],
            "status": "pending_human_collection",
            "items": [
                {"id": "NR01", "annotations": {"A1": None, "A2": None, "A3": None}},
            ],
        }
        aids, by_pair = mod.parse_template_format(data)
        self.assertEqual(aids, ["A1", "A2", "A3"])
        self.assertIsNone(by_pair["NR01"]["A1"])

    def test_null_not_unsure(self):
        data = {
            "annotators": ["A1", "A2", "A3"],
            "items": [
                {
                    "id": "CT01",
                    "annotations": {"A1": "mirrorOf", "A2": "mirrorOf", "A3": None},
                },
            ],
        }
        _, by_pair = mod.parse_template_format(data)
        self.assertIsNone(by_pair["CT01"]["A3"])

    def test_invalid_label_rejected(self):
        data = {
            "annotators": ["A1"],
            "items": [{"id": "X", "annotations": {"A1": "maybe"}}],
        }
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
            mod.parse_template_format(data)


class NoTruncatePolicyTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_heterogeneous_respondent_sets_excluded(self):
        payload = {
            "annotators": ["A1", "A2", "A3", "A4"],
            "status": "partial",
            "items": [
                {
                    "id": "P1",
                    "annotations": {
                        "A1": "none",
                        "A2": "none",
                        "A3": "none",
                        "A4": None,
                    },
                },
                {
                    "id": "P3",
                    "annotations": {
                        "A1": "mirrorOf",
                        "A2": "mirrorOf",
                        "A3": None,
                        "A4": "mirrorOf",
                    },
                },
            ],
        }
        with tempfile.NamedTemporaryFile(
            mode="w", suffix=".json", delete=False, encoding="utf-8"
        ) as fh:
            json.dump(payload, fh)
            path = Path(fh.name)
        try:
            aids, by_pair, _meta = mod.load_annotations(path)
            k_required = 3
            respondent_sets = {}
            for pid, ann_map in by_pair.items():
                respondents = tuple(
                    aid for aid in aids if ann_map.get(aid) is not None
                )
                if len(respondents) == k_required:
                    respondent_sets[pid] = respondents
            self.assertEqual(respondent_sets["P1"], ("A1", "A2", "A3"))
            self.assertEqual(respondent_sets["P3"], ("A1", "A2", "A4"))
            self.assertNotEqual(respondent_sets["P1"], respondent_sets["P3"])
        finally:
            path.unlink(missing_ok=True)


class FleissTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_perfect_agreement(self):
        ratings = [["none", "none", "none"], ["mirrorOf", "mirrorOf", "mirrorOf"]]
        k = mod.fleiss_kappa(ratings, ["none", "mirrorOf", "creates", "unsure"])
        self.assertAlmostEqual(k, 1.0)

    def test_mismatched_lengths_raise(self):
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
            mod.fleiss_kappa(
                [["none", "none"], ["none", "none", "none"]],
                ["none", "mirrorOf"],
            )


class AnnotatorPairsBlindTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_default_pairs_have_no_structural_hints(self):
        path = ROOT / "datasets" / "independent_eval" / "annotator_pairs.csv"
        text = path.read_text(encoding="utf-8").lower()
        forbidden = [
            "fase",
            "posición",
            "posicion",
            "lateral",
            "centro",
            "espejo",
            "creates",
            "mirror",
            "avance",
            "retroceso",
            "canónico",
            "canonico",
            "vocabulario",
            "13 filas",
            "ajeno",
            "new_concept",
            "out_of_rules",
        ]
        for word in forbidden:
            self.assertNotIn(
                word,
                text,
                f"annotator_pairs.csv no debe contener la pista {word!r}",
            )

    def test_nc_items_use_generic_context(self):
        path = ROOT / "datasets" / "independent_eval" / "annotator_pairs.csv"
        lines = {
            row.split(",")[0]: row
            for row in path.read_text(encoding="utf-8").strip().splitlines()[1:]
        }
        for nid in ("NC01", "NC02", "NC03"):
            self.assertIn(nid, lines)
            self.assertIn("Par de conceptos", lines[nid])
            self.assertNotIn("vocabulario", lines[nid].lower())
            self.assertNotIn("13", lines[nid])


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()

FILE: tests/test_validate_spec.py
================================================
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Validación del schema y de la spec canónica."""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import unittest
from copy import deepcopy
from pathlib import Path

from jsonschema import Draft202012Validator

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
SCHEMA = json.loads((ROOT / "data" / "iom_spec.schema.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
SPEC = json.loads((ROOT / "data" / "iom_spec.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))


class SpecificationSchemaTests(unittest.TestCase):
    @classmethod
    def setUpClass(cls):
        cls.validator = Draft202012Validator(SCHEMA)

    def test_canonical_spec_conforms_to_schema(self):
        errors = list(self.validator.iter_errors(SPEC))
        self.assertEqual(errors, [])

    def test_canonical_position_metadata_and_triad_indexing_are_accepted(self):
        spec = deepcopy(SPEC)
        spec["positions"]["indices"] = [0, 1, 2]
        spec["positions"]["adv_labels"] = ["→", "0", "→"]
        spec["positions"]["ret_labels"] = ["←", "0", "←"]
        spec["positions"]["perspectives"] = ["Ind", "D", "Tot"]
        spec["triads"]["index_base"] = 1
        spec["triads"]["note"] = "Los arrays empiezan en cero; triad_index es 1-based."
        errors = list(self.validator.iter_errors(spec))
        self.assertEqual(errors, [])

    def test_invalid_position_metadata_is_rejected(self):
        invalid_spec = deepcopy(SPEC)
        invalid_spec["positions"]["indices"] = [0, 1]
        errors = list(self.validator.iter_errors(invalid_spec))
        self.assertTrue(any(list(error.absolute_path)[:1] == ["positions"] for error in errors))

    def test_unknown_triad_metadata_is_rejected(self):
        spec = deepcopy(SPEC)
        spec["triads"]["unexpected"] = True
        errors = list(self.validator.iter_errors(spec))
        self.assertTrue(any(list(error.absolute_path) == ["triads"] for error in errors))

    def test_missing_spec_version_is_rejected(self):
        invalid_spec = deepcopy(SPEC)
        del invalid_spec["spec_version"]
        errors = list(self.validator.iter_errors(invalid_spec))
        self.assertTrue(any("spec_version" in error.message for error in errors))

    def test_malformed_semantic_version_is_rejected(self):
        invalid_spec = deepcopy(SPEC)
        invalid_spec["spec_version"] = "1.1"
        errors = list(self.validator.iter_errors(invalid_spec))
        self.assertTrue(any(list(error.absolute_path) == ["spec_version"] for error in errors))

    def test_purpose_is_present_and_matches_schema(self):
        """La unidad informativa queda registrada en el spec canónico."""
        self.assertIn("purpose", SPEC)
        purpose = SPEC["purpose"]
        self.assertEqual(purpose["id"], "informational_unit_creation")
        self.assertIn("mínima estructura autocontenida", purpose["definition"])
        self.assertIn("criterion", purpose)
        errors = list(self.validator.iter_errors(SPEC))
        self.assertEqual(errors, [])

    def test_generated_from_records_purpose_id(self):
        stamp = json.loads((ROOT / "datasets" / "GENERATED_FROM.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        self.assertEqual(stamp.get("purpose_id"), "informational_unit_creation")
        self.assertEqual(stamp.get("spec_version"), SPEC["spec_version"])


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()

FILE: tests/optional/test_pi_hgat_t.py

"""Pruebas rápidas de la implementación PI-HGAT-T y su vínculo ontológico."""
import unittest
import torch

from experiments.optional.pi_hgat_t import PIHGATT, admissible_relation, features_for_node, read_data


class PIHGATTTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_feature_vector_uses_only_phase_and_position(self):
        self.assertEqual(features_for_node("T12_adv_P0"), [1.0, 0.0, 1.0, 0.0, 0.0])
        self.assertEqual(features_for_node("T07_ret_P2"), [0.0, 1.0, 0.0, 0.0, 1.0])

    def test_loaders_use_public_triad_ids_one_through_thirteen(self):
        nodes, pairs = read_data()
        expected = set(range(1, 14))
        self.assertEqual(set(nodes), expected)
        self.assertEqual(set(pairs), expected)
        self.assertTrue(all(len(pairs[triad_id]) == 30 for triad_id in expected))

    def test_attention_model_returns_three_relation_logits_per_candidate(self):
        model = PIHGATT(dim=16, heads=4)
        graph = torch.eye(5)[:5].unsqueeze(0)
        pairs = torch.tensor([[0, 1], [1, 0]], dtype=torch.long)
        self.assertEqual(tuple(model(graph, pairs).shape), (1, 2, 3))

    def test_admissibility_uses_mirror_and_creation_properties(self):
        # Índices locales 0..2=avance; 3..5=retroceso.
        self.assertTrue(admissible_relation("mirrorOf", 0, 5))
        self.assertFalse(admissible_relation("mirrorOf", 0, 3))
        self.assertTrue(admissible_relation("creates", 0, 4))
        self.assertTrue(admissible_relation("creates", 4, 0))
        self.assertFalse(admissible_relation("creates", 0, 3))


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()

FILE: .github/workflows/ci.yml

name: CI

on:
  push:
  pull_request:

permissions:
  contents: read

# Único workflow de validez del repositorio.
# Encuentro (EXP-A/E, PI-HGAT-T) vive en workflows/encuentro.yml (manual).

jobs:
  lean:
    name: Lean 4 build (SelfGenesis)
    runs-on: ubuntu-24.04
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v7
      - uses: leanprover/lean-action@v1
        with:
          build: true

  core:
    name: Núcleo autónomo (punto fijo, SHACL, invariancia)
    runs-on: ubuntu-24.04
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v7
      - uses: actions/setup-python@v7
        with:
          python-version: '3.12'
          cache: pip
          cache-dependency-path: requirements-lock.txt
      - name: Install locked dependencies
        run: |
          python -m pip install --upgrade pip
          python -m pip install -r requirements-lock.txt
      - name: Validate iom_spec.json against JSON Schema
        run: python scripts/validate_spec.py
      - name: Check specification migrations
        run: python scripts/migrate_spec.py --check
      - name: Generate ontology/CSV and require versioned CSV match
        run: python scripts/check_generated_committed.py
      - name: Punto fijo idempotente (CSV + sello spec_version)
        run: python scripts/self_bootstrap.py --passes 2
      - name: Check spec ↔ CSV sync
        run: python scripts/sync_spec_artifacts.py --check
      - name: Regenerar TTL (generador + materialize determinista)
        run: |
          python scripts/generate_ontology.py
          python scripts/materialize_ttl.py
      - name: Unit tests (ontología, reglas, schema)
        run: |
          python -m unittest \
            tests.test_formal_rules_bridge \
            tests.test_metrics_audit \
            tests.test_ontology \
            tests.test_property_based \
            tests.test_property_links \
            tests.test_score_independent_eval \
            tests.test_validate_spec \
            -v
      - name: OWL RL + SHACL
        run: python scripts/validate_shacl.py
      - name: Invariancia no trivial (+ control negativo)
        run: python scripts/measure_invariance.py

name: Encuentro (manual)

# No forma parte del estado de validez del commit.
# Disparo manual: dominio, κ histórico, PI-HGAT-T / baselines.

on:
  workflow_dispatch:

permissions:
  contents: read

jobs:
  encounter:
    name: EXP-A / EXP-E / diagnóstico sintético
    runs-on: ubuntu-24.04
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v7
      - uses: actions/setup-python@v7
        with:
          python-version: '3.12'
          cache: pip
          cache-dependency-path: requirements-lock.txt
      - name: Install locked dependencies
        run: |
          python -m pip install --upgrade pip
          python -m pip install -r requirements-lock.txt
      - name: Score independent evaluation (histórico; sin ACCESS_LOG)
        run: python scripts/score_independent_eval.py --annotations datasets/independent_eval/annotations.json
      - name: PI-HGAT-T (diagnóstico circular)
        run: python experiments/optional/pi_hgat_t.py
      - name: Baselines
        run: python experiments/optional/baselines.py
      - name: EXP-E domain mapping
        run: python experiments/domain_mapping/validate_examples.py
